**19.1 (server skeleton + transports + SDK adoption) implemented 2026-07-10.** New `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/` package: `server.go` (SDK `*mcp.Server` wrapper; `advertisedCapabilities()` pins the handshake to exactly tools+resources+prompts, suppressing the SDK's default logging cap — tools/resources advertise `listChanged`, prompts do not), `stdio.go` (SDK `IOTransport`), `http.go` (`ResolveHTTPAddr` refuses non-loopback IPs + any hostname incl. `localhost`; stateless `StreamableHTTPHandler` on `/mcp`; graceful `Shutdown`), `run.go` (`Options`/`Run` orchestrate stdio+HTTP concurrently, `context.Canceled` → clean exit; `NewLogger`). New `cli/cmd_mcp.go` wires `sqlgen mcp serve` with the §3.1 flag set (`--manifest`/`--stdio`/`--http`/`--watch`/`--no-watch`/`--log`/`--log-level`); SIGINT via `signal.NotifyContext`; logs to stderr (stdout reserved for JSON-RPC). **SDK pinned `go-sdk v1.6.1`, scoped to `cmd/sqlgen/go.mod` only** (runtime + parser go.mod byte-unchanged). Tests (all under `-race`): handshake capability-set replay, `ResolveHTTPAddr` 15-case loopback table, transport selection + graceful-shutdown table (stdio/http/coexist), `NewLogger` levels, CLI flag-parse refusals (ExitConfig). `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (example output byte-unchanged — MCP is not in the generation path). **Threaded-but-not-yet-consumed:** `--manifest`/`--watch` wire in 19.2 with the Store + fsnotify watcher. **`/verify 19.1` + `/done 19.1` complete (2026-07-10):** 7/7 PRD PASS, 4/4 tests present, reviewer full agreement (no race / no leak / SDK out of runtime+parser), one `http.go` doc-comment nit fixed inline, 0 FIXes logged.

**19.4 (4 resources + 3 prompts) implemented 2026-07-10.** New `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/resources.go` — three fixed resources via `AddResource` (`sqlgen://manifest`, `sqlgen://conventions`, `sqlgen://config`) + `sqlgen://entity/{name}` as an RFC 6570 `AddResourceTemplate` (concrete reads route via the SDK template match; listed under `resources/templates/list`, not `resources/list`). `sqlgen://manifest` serves the raw bytes verbatim and attaches a size `warning` above `manifestWarnThreshold` (256 KB); `sqlgen://config` surfaces `generation_config` with `Store.GenerationConfig() (cfg, present)` probing the **raw** bytes for the key (a pre-addendum manifest decodes to a zero-valued all-false struct indistinguishable from a genuine all-off config → `{}` + warning fallback); `sqlgen://entity/<name>` returns `-32003` + suggestions on an unknown name. New `prompts.go` — the 3 §4.3 templates (`sqlgen-write-query`/`sqlgen-add-relationship-usage`/`sqlgen-debug-not-found`) as `text/template` bodies expanding to a single user turn that instructs the LLM to pull the relevant surface via sqlgen tools (stateless, no store dependency; required-arg validation → `-32602`; registered once, never re-registered → no `prompts/list_changed`). `server.go` `onManifestReload` now re-registers resources (fires `resources/list_changed`); `run.go` registers resources + prompts before serve. **Two SDK-shape adaptations flagged for `/verify`:** (1) the §4.2 `warning` sibling rides in `_meta.warning` — the SDK's `ResourceContents` struct has no literal `warning` field; body stays byte-pure. (2) `sqlgen://entity/<name>` unknown-name is a genuine `*jsonrpc.Error` (wire code `-32003` + `data.suggestions`) with suggestions **also inlined into the message** — the resource analogue of 19.3's tool-IsError problem: the go-sdk *client* collapses coded errors, and resources have no IsError escape hatch, so the protocol error is the only semantically-correct option and the inline keeps suggestions LLM-visible (wire shape verified by the direct-handler test; 19.6 raw-JSON-RPC leg is the integration check). Tests (`-race`, `resources_test.go` + `prompts_test.go`): per-URI body shape, 256 KB warning (small→none, over→`_meta.warning` + full body), config present-vs-absent fallback, entity found/unknown (+ session-stays-usable), 3-template expansion + `method` branch, required-arg `-32602`, unknown-prompt path, and SDK round-trips (`resources/list`=3, template read, `prompts/list`=3). `make check` clean (0 lint, all unit tests `-race`); `make check-examples` **not** triggered (all changes under `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/**`, not in the path-based E2E table); no security-review trigger. **`/done 19.4` complete (2026-07-10):** auto-review 7/7 PRD PASS, 2/2 test groups, 0 high-confidence issues — the one actionable low-sev note (no wire-level test for `_meta.warning`) closed inline (`TestDispatchManifestWarningSurvivesToClient`); marked complete on the reviewer's ready-for-done verdict without a separate `/verify` (both SDK-shape adaptations faithful + verified against SDK source). 0 FIXes logged. **Next:** `/implement 19.5` (`.mcp.json` emission + `manifest.mcp` config block + agent-integration docs).

**19.3 (the 11 read-only tools + Levenshtein fuzzy suggestions) implemented 2026-07-10.** New `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/` files: `tools.go` (registry + a generic `addTool[In,Out]` adapter — SDK output type `any` so tool-business errors render as `IsError` results and successes marshal the concrete output; re-registered on reload to fire `tools/list_changed`), `tools_entity.go` (`list_entities`/`get_entity`/`find_method`), `tools_graph.go` (`find_referencing`/`describe_relationship`/`find_join_path` — bounded simple-path DFS, `max_hops` default 4 clamped `[1,6]`, ≤5 shortest-first + lexicographic tie-break, cycle-free), `tools_sql.go` (`show_sql` reading `methods[].sql_bodies`; `-32005` when absent/dialect-missing, `-32006` unknown method), `tools_meta.go` (`get_conventions`/`get_example`), `tools_diag.go` (`health`/`validate_manifest` — flattens `*jsonschema.ValidationError` to `{field,msg}`), `suggest.go` (Levenshtein-≤2, case-insensitive, top-3, distance-then-lexicographic). `store.go` gained full-entity resolution (inline for `single`, sibling `entities/` files for `per_entity`) + `Entities()`/`EntityByName`/`EntityByTable`/`EntityNames` accessors under the same atomic swap. **Error-model decision:** per-tool business errors (`-32003..-32006`, `-32603`) surface as MCP **`IsError` tool results** with a structured `{code, message, suggestions}` envelope — the go-sdk client collapses a coded JSON-RPC error returned from a tool handler into an opaque "connection closed" transport error, dropping the code + suggestions the agent needs; IsError keeps them visible (§5.3's intent) and the session stays usable. `-32001`/`-32002` remain genuine load-time errors. `compact` on `get_entity` deep-copies before trimming (no mutation of the shared record). Tests (`-race`): per-tool table-driven over single/composite-PK + tenanted + view + m2m + soft-deleted fixtures, join-path hops/cycle/clamp/tie-break/cap, suggest threshold/case/cap/tie/empty, health transitions, validate in-memory-vs-path, `TestDispatch_Deterministic` (independent stores → byte-equal), and an in-memory SDK round-trip (`tools_dispatch_test.go`) asserting 11 tools register/list, structured content on success, and IsError+envelope on not-found. `make check` clean; `make check-examples` **not** triggered (all changes under `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/**`, not in the path-based E2E table — MCP is not in the generation path; full example coverage lands in 19.6). Watch-reload `tools/list_changed` emission now live (closes the 19.2 tools deferral; resources land in 19.4). **`/verify 19.3` + `/done 19.3` complete (2026-07-10):** 12/12 PRD (MCP.md §4.1/§4.4/§5.3/§6.5) PASS, 8/8 tests present, reviewer full agreement (error-model IsError adaptation faithful to §5.3, determinism via explicit sorts, module boundary clean). One latent determinism nit surfaced + resolved same day — **FIX-130** (`find_referencing`'s `lessReference` sort omitted `Kind` while dedup keyed on the full `Reference`; widened `lessReference` + `findMethod` comparators to match their dedup keys + `TestFindReferencingKindTiebreak`; auto-review 3/3 PASS, no golden churn). Three §31 doc-reconciliation items (get_example compact no-op; `suggestions` flattened vs §5.3 `data`; `find_referencing` `kind` semantics) carried to 19.7 PRD §31 wording — doc-only. 0 open FIXes. Churn-watch still holds: `get_conventions`/`get_entity` read the deferred-freeze conventions sub-blocks, pinned to `0.1.0`. **Next:** `/implement 19.4` (4 resources + 3 prompts; churn-watch: `sqlgen://conventions` reads the deferred-freeze conventions sub-blocks — pin to `0.1.0`).

**19.2 (manifest loader + fsnotify watch) complete 2026-07-10.** New `cmd/sqlgen/mcp/store.go` (`Store`: `sync.RWMutex` + `*manifest.Document` runtime read type — the §6.3 sketch's `*manifest.Manifest` doesn't exist; `Load`/`Reload` = read → `ValidateAgainstSchema` → unmarshal → atomic swap; tracks `loadedAt`/`lastReloadAt`/`ok` health state; `DiscoverManifest` walks up from CWD for `manifest_gen.json`), `watch.go` (fsnotify watcher on the **directory** not the inode — survives editor/atomic rename-replace; debounced burst-coalescing; failed reload keeps last-good + flips `health.ok=false`, no `onReload`), `errors.go` (coded `*Error`: `-32001`/`-32002` + `-32603` internal). New `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/validate.go` (`ValidateAgainstSchema` — offline embedded-schema validation, no `$schema` fetch, the reload hot path). `run.go` wires discovery + initial `Store.Load` (Run now hard-fails without a manifest per §3.1) + the watcher as a `WaitGroup` goroutine torn down on ctx cancel; a `NewWatcher` setup failure degrades to no-watch rather than aborting. **`fsnotify v1.9.0` added as a direct dep, `cmd/sqlgen/go.mod` only** (runtime + parser go.mod byte-unchanged). Tests (`-race`): store validation/atomic-swap/version-mismatch/failed-reload-retains-good/discovery; watch reload-on-write/rename-replace/debounce/no-fire-on-invalid. `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (example output byte-unchanged — MCP + validator not in the generation path). Auto-review 0 blocking / 3 low-sev (2 fixed inline: internal-vs-not-found read-error code, watcher-setup graceful degradation); the one PARTIAL is the **watch-reload notification emission** — a wired seam (`Server.onManifestReload`) that emits nothing until 19.3/19.4 register tool/resource surfaces (the SDK fires `list_changed` only on registry mutation), verified end-to-end by the 19.6 watch subtest. `/done 19.2` complete; 0 FIXes logged. **Next:** `/implement 19.3` (the 11 read-only tools + Levenshtein fuzzy suggestions; churn-watch: `get_conventions`/`get_entity` read the deferred-freeze `conventions` sub-blocks — pin to `0.1.0`).

**Phase 18 (Manifest) closed 2026-07-09.** All 10 sub-items complete: 18.1 `manifest:` config block + 8 validation rules (§30.2/§4.13); 18.2 builder package + the GraphQL `.graphqls` description-wiring side-effect (§26.4); 18.3 deterministic JSON emission (single/per_entity) + `schema/v1.json`; 18.4 markdown emission (`_index`/`_conventions`/entity templates); 18.5 agent breadcrumbs (`CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`) + provenance-marker ownership; 18.6 pipeline wiring + stale cleanup + `--manifest-timestamp`; 18.7 stdlib-only runtime `manifest/` package + `Client.Manifest()`/`ManifestEntity()`/`ManifestVersion()`; 18.8 CLI subcommands `manifest validate` + `diff`; 18.9 E2E manifest surface across all four example dialects; 18.10 closure sweep (this entry).

**18.10 closure sweep (landed 2026-07-09):** `make check` clean across all 8 modules under `-race` (longest unit legs ~2.2s; cmd/sqlgen subpackages green); `make check-examples` clean across all example modules under `-race` (longest legs graphql/tests ~35.0s, mysql/tests ~10.1s, postgres/tests ~4.9s); `make test-integration` clean, testcontainers up (longest legs cmd/sqlgen/cli ~393s, cmd/sqlgen E2E ~181s, gen ~31s; ~7:09 wall) — no flakes across the sweep. **FIX triage:** 0 open at closure; the entire Phase 18 FIX batch (FIX-113…FIX-129, incl. the carried-over FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120 and the 18.9-surfaced blocking FIX-127/128/129) is Resolved; 0 carried forward, 0 must-resolve outstanding. **Deferred by user direction:** the JSON Schema **v1.0.0 freeze** — `schema_version` is held at `0.1.0`, so the paired 18.10 pre-freeze tasks (tighten `schema/v1.json` conventions `required` + shape the four always-emitted sub-blocks `call_options`/`soft_delete`/`comparator`/`omittable`; tag `schema_version: 1.0.0`) are carried forward together as a single follow-up to run when the version bump is greenlit. No golden churn this closure (no schema/version change). `docs/design/MANIFEST.md` already SYNCED (2026-05-15, superseded by PRD §30). **Surface delta (across Phase 18):** the manifest is a new, opt-in, purely-additive generated surface — a small, deterministic, structured description of the generated package written for both AI agents (on-disk JSON + markdown + auto-discovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` breadcrumbs) and Go consumers (runtime-embedded typed structs on the generated `Client`). It adds the `generation.manifest` config block (+ per-table opt-out), a stdlib-only runtime `manifest/` package (~30 types), the `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/` builder+emitter (JSON in single/per_entity layouts, markdown, breadcrumbs, `schema/v1.json`), two new CLI subcommands (`sqlgen manifest validate` + `diff`), and wires GraphQL `.graphqls` type/field descriptions through `Table.Comment`/`Column.Comment`. Nothing changes in generated Go behavior when `manifest.enabled` is false. **Next:** run the deferred v1.0.0 freeze pair when the version bump is approved; Phase 18 is otherwise feature-complete.

**Phase 18.9 (E2E manifest example surface across all dialects) landed 2026-07-09.** All four examples (`postgres`/`mysql`/`sqlite` `single`, `graphql` `per_entity`) now enable `generation.manifest`; the E2E harness (`e2e_test.go`) threads a fixed `--manifest-timestamp` so `generated_at` is byte-deterministic. Extended-metadata fixtures: composite indexes (all four), partial indexes (pg/sqlite `WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`, round-trips into the manifest index `where` field), and graphql `COMMENT ON categories` — which exercises the §26.4 `.graphqls`-description wiring E2E for the first time (comment flows to manifest + Go doc comments + `graph/category_gen.graphqls`). Golden trees regenerated for every manifest artifact (JSON/MD, breadcrumbs, `manifest_embed_gen.go`, graphql `entities/` + `graph/` breadcrumbs, sqlite per-table split). Tests: 4 runtime/Client sub-tests per example (`manifest_test.go`: on-disk shape, `Client.Manifest()` round-trip, `ManifestEntity` lookup, `ManifestVersion` agreement); schema-validation + `manifest validate` CLI over REAL orchestrated output in `cli.TestManifestValidate_E2EExamples` (**placed in `cmd/sqlgen/cli` not the example `tests/` — the embedded schema + `jsonschema/v5` are unreachable across the root-module-only example boundary**); `manifest/load_test.go::TestLoadInto_SubRootedFS` (closes the `/verify 18.7` `fs.Sub` fidelity gap). **Two blocking bugs surfaced by 18.9's new guards + fixed in-scope:** **FIX-128** — view entities emitted `indexes: null` (nil slice) vs the schema's array type; caught by the first real-output schema check (the FIX-068/FIX-059 divergence shape); `buildViewEntity` now emits `[]Index{}`. **FIX-129** — `output.layout: file_per_table` (used by the sqlite example to guard the 18.6 `manifest_embed_gen.go` stale-sweep ordering) shipped non-compiling output: per-file emission used `FormatOnly` (gofmt, no import resolution) on incomplete table-context imports, so `context`/`comparator`/`sql`/… were never back-filled (never caught — no example had ever used `file_per_table`). Fixed via `resolvePackageImports` (one goimports scan over the concatenated bodies → complete set) + a normal `Format` per file that, with the complete set already seeded, finds nothing missing (no per-file scan) and only prunes — correct AND keeps per-file speed (~one scan regardless of table count; user-directed over goimports-per-file). **FIX-127 resolved (2026-07-09):** CHECK constraints now surface in the manifest E2E. Root cause was builder-side — the dialect parsers populate `Constraint.CheckExpression` but never `Constraint.Columns` for checks, so the per-column flatten predicate never matched. Fixed via `checkConstraintColumns`, which derives the participating columns from the CHECK expression text (a lightweight identifier tokenizer that skips string literals and handles bare/quoted identifiers) when `Columns` is empty. Re-added table-level `CHECK (unit_price >= 0 AND quantity > 0)` fixtures on pg/mysql/sqlite `order_items`; goldens regenerated (`check` flattens onto `quantity` + `unit_price`). The three dialects exercise all tokenizer paths (bare / vitess-lowercased `and` / rqlite double-quoted). `/fix-implement FIX-127` + follow-up sqlite fixture: `make check` + `make check-examples` clean; independent reviewer 0 blocking. **`/verify 18.9` (2026-07-09):** 6/6 PRD PASS, 9/9 required tests present, `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (Docker), `TestManifestValidate_E2EExamples` green across all four examples; independent reviewer full agreement, 0 blocking; 0 FIXes logged. **`/done 18.9` (2026-07-09).** **Next:** 18.10 (phase closure sweep + PRD sync) — gated on the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120), now that FIX-127 is resolved.

**Phase 18.8 (CLI subcommands `sqlgen manifest validate` + `diff`) landed 2026-07-09.** New `manifest` parent command (`cmd/sqlgen/cli/cmd_manifest.go`) with two on-disk, no-runtime-dependency subcommands (PRD §30.8). **`validate <path>`** (`cmd_manifest_validate.go`): read → parse → resolve schema (fetch the manifest's `$schema` URL, fall back to the embedded `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/schema/v1.json` on any fetch/compile failure) → `jsonschema/v5` validate → one-line summary (`schema_version`, entity/enum/extra counts, layout-agnostic). Exit 0 success / 1 schema-or-parse failure / 2 I/O failure (git-style, distinct from root.go's generation codes). Network fetch is scheme-allowlisted (http/https), 10s-timeout-bounded, 4 MiB size-capped, and injectable via `manifestSchemaFetcher` for hermetic tests. **`diff <old> <new>` [`--json`]** (`cmd_manifest_diff.go`): layout-aware loader (follows `entities[].file` pointers in per_entity — guarded by `withinDir` against `../` traversal — re-reads inline `entities[]` in single) → structured diff over entities/columns/methods/sentinels/features/generation_config via a uniform `setDiff{added,removed,changed}` + `kvChange{key,old,new}` model. Human output (one section per dimension) or deterministic `--json` (all slices sorted, HTML-escaping off). Exit 0 identical / 1 differ (non-printed `errManifestDiff` sentinel + `SilenceErrors`). `jsonschema/v5` promoted indirect→direct in `cmd/sqlgen/go.mod`; README "Manifest tooling" section documents both commands + exit-code table + `--json` shape. **Verification:** `/verify 18.8` 8/8 stated PRD acceptance criteria PASS, 19/19 required tests present (7 validate + 12 diff), `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (change is CLI-only — subcommands never run during example generation, so example output byte-unchanged, no golden regen); independent reviewer full agreement, 0 blocking. **FIX triage:** 1 tracked logged + resolved before `/done` — **FIX-126** (`manifest diff` under-detected column/method *changes*: `columnSig` compared only Go/DB type + nullability, `methodSig` omitted `errors[]`, so a PK/UNIQUE/default flip or error-set change on an otherwise-unchanged column/method surfaced as no diff). Resolved via `/fix-implement FIX-126` (2026-07-09): widened `columnSig` (adds `null`/`pk`/`unique`/`default=…`/`default_kind=…`/`check=…`/`comparator=…`) + `methodSig` (sorted `errors=[…]` so a reorder is not a spurious diff) + 3 regression tests (`TestManifestDiff_ColumnConstraintFlipped`, `_MethodErrorSetChanged`, `_MethodErrorReorderNotChanged`); `make check` clean, `cmd/sqlgen/cli`-only, no golden/example impact. **Deferred to phase-18 `/security-review`** (no FIX): SSRF-hardening residual on `validate`'s `$schema` fetch (redirects followed, no private-IP/link-local block) — PRD-inherent (§30.8 mandates fetching the `$schema` URL) + low-severity (the `$schema` is a builder-set constant in sqlgen-generated manifests; the fetched body is used only as a schema, never returned to the caller). **Next:** 18.9 (E2E example surface across all dialects) — or the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120) the closure sweep still gates on.

**Phase 18.6 (Manifest pipeline wiring + stale cleanup) landed 2026-07-09.** The manifest stage is now wired into `sqlgen generate`. **Placement deviation from the phase doc:** the doc sketched this into `cmd/sqlgen/gen/orchestrate.go`, but `cmd/sqlgen/manifest` imports `cmd/sqlgen/gen` (contexts + `BuildManifestEmbedContext`), so calling `manifest.Build`/`Emit*` from inside `gen.Generate` would form an **import cycle**. Resolved by coordinating from the **CLI** (the one layer importing both) via a new `manifest.RunStage(StageInput) ([]string, error)` called at `generate.go` step 7c — after gen emission, gen's `CleanStaleFiles`, and the chained GraphQL step (so `manifest_embed_gen.go`, a `_gen.go` file gen doesn't track, survives the file_per_table stale sweep). `RunStage`: gate on `manifestEnabled` → `Build` → `EmitJSON`/`EmitMarkdown` (each gated by `formats`) → `EmitBreadcrumbs` → `gen.BuildManifestEmbedContext(cfg, len(doc.Entities))` + new exported `gen.RenderManifestEmbed` (wraps the package-private `renderAndWrite("manifest-embed", …)`) → `CleanStale`; disabled path runs `CleanStale` only. **`GenerateResult` now returns the in-memory `Tables`/`Views`/`API` contexts** (and `generateAPI` returns its `*APIContext`) so the manifest reuses the exact contexts that produced `_gen.go` — no re-parse. (`BuildInput.API` is currently unread by the builder — wired for future-proofing.) `manifest.CleanStale` (new `clean.go`, PRD §23.8/§30.3): disabled → `os.RemoveAll` the whole `<markdown_dir>/` (**superseded by FIX-210** — the unconditional `RemoveAll` is now ownership-gated on proof a prior enabled run wrote the directory; it removed consumer-owned `manifest/` directories otherwise) + marker-gated breadcrumb deletion (output + layered graph dir, via 18.5's exported `BreadcrumbIsSqlgenOwned`) + `manifest_embed_gen.go`; enabled → per-flag breadcrumb opt-out, embed removal when `embed_in_client:false`, glob-prune orphaned per-entity `*.md`/`*.json` (single layout wipes the whole `entities/` dir). **`--manifest-timestamp`** flag (deferred from 18.3) on root + `generate`: `resolveManifestTimestamp` → flag verbatim else `time.Now().UTC()` RFC3339 → `StageInput.Timestamp` → `doc.GeneratedAt`. Graph-package breadcrumbs driven by `ResolveGraphBreadcrumbDir` — emitted whenever GraphQL is enabled and the graph dir is distinct from the output dir (nested **or** top-level sibling; FIX-124), not layered-only. Tests (**relocated** to `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/emit_test.go` since the stage lives in the manifest pkg): 8 `RunStage`/`CleanStale` cases (disabled-by-default, per-table opt-out, full artifact set, removed-table both layouts, enabled-flip sweep, breadcrumb opt-out, marker-gated user-file preservation, timestamp determinism) + `cli.TestResolveManifestTimestamp`. **Verification:** `make check` clean (all 8 modules, `-race`); `make check-examples` clean (no example enables manifest → `RunStage` runs the disabled/cleanup path → example output byte-unchanged, no golden regen). **Security-review not fired** (no new external-file/URL reader; runtime `//go:embed manifest/` untouched) — formal `/security-review` deferred to `/verify 18.6`. **Auto-review: 8/8 in-scope PRD PASS, 9/9 tests, guidelines PASS; one high-confidence finding fixed in-place** — a disabled config with explicit `markdown_dir: "."` resolved `markdownDir` back to the output dir, so the disabled-path `os.RemoveAll` would have wiped the whole output tree (disabled-path validation doesn't reject `markdown_dir: "."`). Fixed with a containment guard (`removeManifestDir`/`within`) on both `RemoveAll` sites + `TestCleanStale_GuardsAgainstOutputDirWipe`. **No FIX logged.** **Deferred to 18.9:** (1) `breadcrumbs.package_doc` → `models_gen.go` doc-comment pointer — the sole remaining task item; touches gen's shared header path (`WrapWithPreamble`/`injectHeader`), golden-churn risk, no 18.6 test, wants the E2E vehicle. (2) `diff` manifest-artifact preview (non-manifest diff output unaffected). **Next:** 18.8 (CLI subcommands `manifest validate` + `diff`) — or the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120) the closure sweep still gates on.

**Phase 18.7 (Runtime-embedded manifest + Go types package) landed 2026-07-09.** New top-level runtime package `manifest/` at repo root (`github.com/teandresmith/sqlgen/manifest` — peer to `database/`/`cache/`/`comparator/`/`omittable/`, **stdlib-only**, verified by `deps_test.go`'s `go list -deps` check). `types.go` exports ~30 named types mirroring the canonical manifest JSON (PRD §30.4/§30.4.1/§30.4.2/§30.4.3/§30.7) + typed aliases `Layout`/`Dialect`/`EntityKind`/`RelationshipKind` (each `~string` + `String()` + a constant set) + `EntityIndex`. **Key asymmetry vs the builder-side `cmd/sqlgen/manifest`:** runtime `Document.Entities` is `[]EntityIndex` (lightweight, per PRD §30.6 "Entities[] is always the lightweight index"); full records reach consumers via `entityMap`, not `Document.Entities`. `errors.go`: `ErrEntityNotFound` + `ErrParseManifest`. `load.go`: `LoadInto(topJSON []byte, entityFS fs.FS, dst *Document, entityMap map[string]*Entity) error` — per-entity layout walks `entityFS` (`fs.WalkDir`, decode each `*.json` keyed by `Entity.Table`); single layout (nil `entityFS`) recovers full inline records via a **second decode** of `topJSON` through a `struct{ Entities []Entity }` view; all failures multi-`%w`-wrapped in `ErrParseManifest`. Generator side: `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/manifest_embed.go.tmpl` (the `manifest-embed` template — embeds top-level JSON + `all:` entities subtree into one `embed.FS`, `init()` reads/`fs.Sub`-scopes/`LoadInto`s, defines the three `Client` methods `Manifest()`/`ManifestEntity(table)`/`ManifestVersion()`) + `context_manifest.go` (`BuildManifestEmbedContext` → nil when manifest off / `embed_in_client: false`). **Embed-into-`embed.FS` (not bare `[]byte`)** is deliberate: a directive-only `[]byte`+`import "embed"` loses its `embed` import to `renderAndWrite`'s `goimports` pass — would break real generated output, not just the test. Template auto-registers via the existing `templates/*.go.tmpl` glob; the `renderAndWrite` call site is **18.6 stage 9**. Tests: `document_test.go` (Document+Entity round-trip, `GenerationConfig` 18-case decode, `SQLBodies` decode), `load_test.go` (single/per-entity/layout-agnostic/version/error-wrapping via `fstest.MapFS`), `deps_test.go` (stdlib-only), `manifest_embed_test.go` (gating + paths + render-through-`goimports` both layouts). **Design flags for `/verify`:** import path uses real module `tsly-tech` (PRD/§5.9's `teandresmith` is the stale-owner drift 18.3 already corrected); `GeneratedAt string` (not §5.9's non-normative `time.Time`) to stay wire-identical with the builder-side + dodge `cmp.Diff`/`time.Time` friction; `LoadInto` uses `*Document`+`fs.FS` (this task list) not §5.9's superseded `**Document`+`embed.FS`. **Verification:** `make check` clean (all 8 modules; `manifest` ~2.3s, `cmd/sqlgen/gen` ~6.4s); `make check-examples` clean (template registered but never rendered → example output byte-unchanged, no golden regen). **Deferred to 18.9** (all need an orchestrated+compiled example `Client`, which arrives with 18.6 wiring — same shape as 18.3's real-`Build`→emit deferral): reflection method-signature pin (covered at template-render level here), layout-agnostic `Client.ManifestEntity` on a real Client (covered at `LoadInto` layer), `ManifestVersion()`-vs-`SchemaVersion` drift on a real Client (covered at load layer), binary-size regression (<50KB). **Deferred to 18.6:** the `renderAndWrite(tmpl, "manifest-embed", …)` call + `EmbedInClient` gate + `manifest_embed_gen.go` stale-cleanup. **Security-review trigger fired** (new `manifest/` is `//go:embed`-shipped to consumers) — stdlib-only decoder of build-time-trusted JSON; formal `/security-review` deferred to `/verify 18.7`. **No FIX logged.** **Next:** 18.6 (pipeline wiring + stale cleanup) — now unblocked (18.3/18.4/18.5/18.7 all landed) — or continue the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120) that the closure sweep still gates on.

**Phase 18.5 (Manifest breadcrumb files + provenance-marker ownership) landed 2026-07-09.** New `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/emit_breadcrumbs.go` — `EmitBreadcrumbs(BreadcrumbsInput) ([]string, error)` writes the agent-discoverable breadcrumbs (`CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`) into `<output.dir>/` (and, in layered mode, into `GraphDir`). One rendered body written to both paths → byte-identical; each gated by its sub-flag (`breadcrumbs.claude_md` / `agents_md`). `breadcrumb.md.tmpl` (models: `_index.md` header + per-entity pointer list + `_conventions.md` pointer) and `breadcrumb_layered.md.tmpl` (graph: 3-pointer layout — models manifest via `filepath.Rel`+`ToSlash` relative path, sibling `*_gen.graphqls`, PRD §26) both prepend the provenance marker via the shared `{{ marker }}` funcmap (added to `markdownFuncs`, single-source `breadcrumbMarker` const). **Ownership rule (PRD §30.3):** absent → write; present with `Generated by sqlgen` marker in the leading HTML-comment block → overwrite; present without → skip + return a warning (never hard-fail). `hasSqlgenMarker` inspects only the leading comment block, so deleting the marker line disowns the file. `BreadcrumbIsSqlgenOwned(path)` **exported** for 18.6's marker-gated stale cleanup. `ownershipWarning` names the file + offers the marker-free `pointerBlock` + cites the `<flag>: false` opt-out. `emit_breadcrumbs_test.go`: `_Goldens`, `_LayeredGoldens`, `_LayeredRelativePath` (sibling + nested), `_OptOut`, `_Ownership` (4 states), `_MarkerNotInPointerBlock`, `_Determinism` (×4 files). **Verification:** `/verify 18.5` 8/8 in-scope PRD PASS (9th — `package_doc` doc-comment pointer — deferred to 18.6), 7/7 tests present, independent reviewer full agreement (confirmed `hasSqlgenMarker` robust — all ambiguous inputs resolve to the safe user-owned direction), 0 blocking; `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (emitter not yet wired into `orchestrate` — 18.6 — so no example output change). **No FIX logged** — all in-scope requirements satisfied. **Deferred to 18.6** (mirrors 18.3's `--manifest-timestamp` / stale-cleanup deferrals): the `breadcrumbs.package_doc` → `models_gen.go` package-doc-comment manifest pointer (a `cmd/sqlgen/gen` preamble concern with no call site until the manifest stage is wired into `Generate`; 18.6's stale-cleanup task list + acceptance criteria already reference the cleanup side). **Note for 18.6:** its task list references the ownership helper by its pre-implementation unexported name `breadcrumbIsSqlgenOwned` — actual shipped symbol is exported `BreadcrumbIsSqlgenOwned`; and the positive package-doc *injection* task (write the pointer when `package_doc: true`) is currently only implied via the cleanup line, not a distinct task item. **Next:** 18.6 (pipeline wiring + stale cleanup) — or continue the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120) that the closure sweep still gates on.

**Phase 18.4 (Manifest markdown emission) landed 2026-07-09.** New `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/emit_markdown.go` — `EmitMarkdown(doc, mfst, outputDir)` renders the three on-disk markdown artifacts under `<output.dir>/<markdown_dir>/` (default `manifest/`): `_index.md` (header + `Features:` line from `generation_config`, entity scan table, cross-ref to `_conventions.md`), `_conventions.md` (the 7 package-wide reference sections — client entry points/`find_returns_nil_on_missing`, error sentinels, pagination, CallOptions, soft-delete, comparator walkthrough, omittable walkthrough), and one `<file_prefix>.md` per entity (header/files/PK/columns/indexes/CHECK/relationships/query+mutation methods with per-dialect Generated SQL/filter/sort/gated examples). Templates embedded via `//go:embed templates/*.md.tmpl`, parsed once with a pure-deterministic funcmap (`featuresLine`, `sqlBlocks`, `sortedPairs`, `mdCell`, `fkCell`, `checkedColumns`, …) — `text/template` (no HTML escaping), every map projected to a sorted slice (no template ranges a Go map). `normalizeMarkdown` collapses blank-line runs + guarantees one trailing newline but is **fence-aware** (blank lines inside ` ```sql `/` ```go ` fences preserved verbatim). Per-entity filename = `Entity.FilePrefix` verbatim (inherits `<schema>_<table>` multi-schema form). `emit_markdown_test.go`: `TestEmitMarkdown_Goldens` (4 files: `_index`, `_conventions`, `users`, `active_users` view), `_FeaturesLine` (all-on `graph_top_level` rename / all-off `none` / mixed), `_GeneratedSQLBlock` (populated / empty-omit / multi-dialect alphabetical), `_MultiSchemaFilename`, `_Determinism`, `_Examples` (read+write / read-only, mutation-verified fence guard). **Verification:** `/verify 18.4` 7/7 PRD PASS, 5/5 tests present, independent reviewer full agreement, 0 blocking; `make check` + `make check-examples` clean (emitter not yet wired into `orchestrate` — 18.6 — so no example output change). **FIX triage:** 1 tracked logged + resolved before `/done` — **FIX-123** (per-entity examples template branch untested; resolved via `/fix-implement FIX-123` with `TestEmitMarkdown_Examples`, test-only). **Next:** 18.5 (breadcrumb files `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` + package-doc pointer + provenance-marker ownership) — or continue the carried-over Phase 18 fix batch (FIX-115/116/117/118/119/120) that the closure sweep still gates on.

**Phase 20 (GraphQL Output Dirs) closed 2026-07-08.** All 2 sub-items complete: 20.1 made `api.graphql.schema_dir` / `resolver_dir` root-relative (default `<output.dir>/graph`, import wiring, FIX-071 retirement, disabled-API inert); 20.2 added the `graphql_top_level` E2E example proving the top-level sibling `graph/` layout generates + compiles, plus the golden harness's sibling-graph diff.

**20.2 closure sweep (landed 2026-07-08):** `make check` clean across all modules under `-race` (longest leg `cli` ~40s; ~66s wall); `make check-examples` clean across all example modules (longest leg `graphql/tests` ~30s; ~70s wall); `make test-integration` clean (longest legs `cmd/sqlgen` `TestE2EGoldenFiles` ~120s + `cli` ~63s, `parser/introspect` ~9s, `database/stdlib` ~8s; ~155s wall); no flakes. **FIX triage:** 1 resolved during closure — **FIX-121** (blocking, phase 20), the `graphql_top_level` `resolver.go` import-order non-idempotency (fresh scaffold hand-built imports in fixed order and skipped goimports, diverging from the merge path for a top-level sibling layout) + a second defect it surfaced (`fmt-examples` gofumpt-mangling the un-pruned top-level `graph/` generator output); fixed by routing the fresh scaffold through `imports.Process{FormatOnly: true}` (sorts without the module-cache scan) and adding `-name graph` to the `fmt-examples` prune. 6 carried forward to Phase 18 (all Phase 18 `cmd/sqlgen/manifest`, disjoint from Phase 20's graph-dir surface; Phase 18 is separately in progress and owns them): FIX-115 (blocking — `generation_config.soft_delete`/`audit_columns` hardcoded true), FIX-116 (blocking — view entities missing `source`), FIX-117 (tracked — `graph_top_level` inference, explicitly gated on Phase 20 landing, now unblocked), FIX-118/119/120 (tracked — `default_kind` classification, conventions `examples` key, `sql_bodies` INSERT/UPSERT column set). **Surface delta summary (across Phase 20):** `api.graphql.schema_dir` / `resolver_dir` are now root-relative (paths from the module root like `output.dir`), so the generated graph package can live nested (`models/graph`, the default `<output.dir>/graph`) or as a top-level sibling (`graph`) with no mode flag — the `output.dir`-relative `resolveAPIDir` and FIX-071's `output.dir`-prefix import workaround are retired, and disabled-API config (`api.graphql.enabled: false`) is fully inert (no validation, resolution, or emission). A new `graphql_top_level` E2E example proves the sibling layout generates + compiles, and the golden harness (`e2e_test.go`) gained sibling-graph diffing (`siblingGraphDirs` / `extractGraphDirs`) to capture graph trees outside `output.dir`. FIX-121 additionally hardened the one-shot `resolver.go` scaffold to goimports-sort on first write (idempotent across regens) and stopped `fmt-examples` from mangling top-level `graph/` output. **Next:** FIX-117 (Phase 18 manifest infers `generation_config.graph_top_level` from the resolved graph dir, now unblocked) → the rest of the 18.2 fix batch (115/116/118/119/120).

**Phase 20.2 (`graphql_top_level` E2E module + golden-harness sibling-graph diff) landed 2026-07-08.** New E2E example `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql_top_level/` proving the root-relative top-level GraphQL layout (§26.5.8 Approach A) generates + compiles. Schema trimmed to 2 tables (`categories` BIGSERIAL PK; `products` UUID PK, M2O → categories, NUMERIC price) — enough to emit a graph package with one relationship resolver, not to re-prove resolver/walker/connection behavior (the `graphql` example owns that). `sqlgen.yml`: `api.graphql.enabled: true`, `schema_dir: graph` / `resolver_dir: graph` (root-relative → top-level `./graph`, **sibling** of `./models`), global uuid → `uuid.UUID` + numeric → `decimal.Decimal` overrides only (no cache/tenancy/soft-delete). `gqlgen.yml` paths mirror the top-level `graph/`. `go.mod`/`go.sum` derived from the graphql example, module renamed, `go mod tidy` pruned to the real dep set (gqlgen, gqlparser, uuid, pgx, decimal, x/sync, sqlgen replace). `expected/` golden tree holds the models contents at its root **plus** a sibling `graph/` subtree. Generated import paths bake `<module>/graph[/model|/sqlgenresolver]` — NOT `<module>/models/graph` — exercising the FIX-071 retirement end-to-end. `tests/wiring_test.go` (`TestTopLevelGraphWiring`) is a DB-free compile proof: builds the top-level `graph.Resolver{Client,Q,M}` + `ExecutableSchema`. **Harness change (`cmd/sqlgen/e2e_test.go`) — the one non-mechanical piece:** the golden diff previously walked only `output.dir`, so a top-level sibling `graph/` would generate but go **undiffed** (the silent gap 20.1 verification flagged). Added `siblingGraphDirs` + `extractGraphDirs` (line-based, mirroring `apiGraphQLEnabled`): read `api.graphql.schema_dir`/`resolver_dir`, resolve root-relative, return the ones outside `output.dir`; `copyOutputToExpected`/`compareWithExpected` take a `siblings` map and fold each such tree in under its root-relative path (`expected/graph/…`). **Inert for every existing example** — graph nested under `output.dir` (the `graphql` example's `models/graph`) yields an empty map → unchanged behavior, confirmed by a full `TestE2EGoldenFiles` run (all examples byte-clean, 38.7s) + `git status` showing `graphql` untouched. Wiring into `make check-examples` / `TestE2EGoldenFiles` is automatic (wildcard `examples/*/go.mod` + `sqlgen.yml` discovery). **Verification:** new module `go build`/`go vet`/`go test ./tests/` green (GOWORK=off, no Docker); `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql_top_level` + full `TestE2EGoldenFiles` byte-clean; `cmd/sqlgen` vet clean + `go test -short -race ./...` green + `golangci-lint` 0 issues; new module `golangci-lint` 0 issues; `gofumpt -d` clean on all touched handwritten files. `make fmt` deliberately NOT run (its blanket `gofumpt -w .` uses local gofumpt v0.8.0/go1.24.2 vs the golangci-bundled gofumpt/go1.26.0 that captured the goldens — the 16.8g drift trap); handwritten formatting validated via `gofumpt -d`, goldens validated byte-for-byte by the harness. Docker `test-examples` sweep across other (untouched, byte-clean) modules not re-run (new module's test is DB-free; no runtime SQL path touched). No `/security-review` trigger. **Carry-over unchanged:** FIX-117 (Phase 18) still owns `manifest/builder.go` `graph_top_level` inference + the §8 `reservedManifestArtifactDirs` resolved-graph-dir check; 18.9 layers the manifest breadcrumb assertions (design note §10 assertions 3–5) on this topology. **Next:** FIX-117 → the 18.2 fix batch (or Phase 20 closure via `/close-phase 20`).

**Phase 20.1 (root-relative `schema_dir` / `resolver_dir`) landed 2026-07-08.** `api.graphql.schema_dir` / `resolver_dir` are now **root-relative** — paths from the module root, like `output.dir` — instead of `output.dir`-relative (PRD §26.5.8, design PRD §26.5.8). Changes: (1) `config.applyAPIDefaults` defaults unset graph dirs to the computed `filepath.Join(cfg.Output.Dir, "graph")` (i.e. `<output.dir>/graph`, nested alongside models) — a computed value carrying the full path; explicit values taken verbatim. (2) `gen/orchestrate.go` — the `output.dir`-relative `resolveAPIDir` is replaced by `resolveGraphDir(projectRoot, dir)` (abs verbatim, else `filepath.Join(projectRoot, dir)`); new `Options.ProjectRoot` (default `"."`, the cwd = module root sqlgen runs in) is the write anchor so a top-level `schema_dir: graph` lands at `<root>/graph` (sibling of models) rather than under `output.dir`. (3) **FIX-071 retired** — `PopulateAPIContextResolverFields` computes the resolver / `sqlgenresolver` import paths as `JoinModulePath(module, g.ResolverDir[/sqlgenresolver])` directly (no `output.dir` prefix); the default `<output.dir>/graph` already carries the full path, and a top-level `graph` resolves to `<module>/graph` exactly. `ModelsImportPath` still tracks `output.dir` (unchanged), so the graph and models import paths diverge precisely when graph lives outside the models tree. (4) **Disabled-API is inert** — `validateAPIConfig` early-returns when `api.graphql.enabled` is false (no field validated; the now-redundant `g.Enabled &&` on the FIX-077 alias-collision check dropped); `BuildAPIContext` already nil-gates emission. (5) The graphql example's `schema_dir` / `resolver_dir` `./graph` → `./models/graph` (reproduces the identical nested location + import path — goldens byte-identical). **Tests:** `gen/api_orchestrate_test.go` adds `TestResolveGraphDir` (on-disk dir: nested/top-level/custom/abs), `TestGraphImportPaths_RootRelative` (import path with the decisive FIX-071 guard `output.dir=models` + `resolver_dir=graph` → `<module>/graph`, not `<module>/models/graph`), `TestBuildAPIContext_DisabledIsInert`; `config/graphql_dirs_test.go` adds `TestLoadConfig_GraphDirsRootRelativeDefault` (default computation + verbatim) + `TestValidatePreParse_APIGraphQLDisabledIsInert`. **Verification:** `make check` clean (all modules, `-short -race`; gen ~6.9s, config ~1.4s); `make check-examples` clean (all 8 example modules; graphql/tests ~33.5s); `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql` byte-clean in non-short mode; no stray source-tree writes after a full `go test ./cmd/sqlgen/...`. test-integration not re-run — codegen path/import-wiring only, no runtime SQL touched. **Auto-review caught + fixed two items:** (1) HIGH — three chained-graphql cli generate tests (`generate_test.go`) ran from the cli package dir with a relative `schema_dir`, so under root-relative resolution `generate` leaked a `cmd/sqlgen/cli/graph/` tree into the repo (graph is emitted before the gqlgen pre-flight fails); fixed by `t.Chdir`'ing those tests into their temp project root (matches how `sqlgen` runs — from the module root). (2) minor — `validateAPIConfig`'s disabled-gate keyed only on `graphql.enabled` while emission keys on `api.enabled && graphql.enabled`; aligned validation to `!cfg.API.Enabled || !g.Enabled`. **Carry-over:** (a) `manifest/builder.go:157` still uses the pre-§26.5.8 `graph_top_level` heuristic (`SchemaDir != "" && != "."`), now wrong for the root-relative model — correcting it to "resolved graph dir not under `output.dir`" is **FIX-117** (Phase 18), deferred by this phase's scope rule. (b) The golden harness only diffs the `output.dir` subtree, so **20.2** (`graphql_top_level` E2E module) owns any harness wiring needed to capture a sibling `graph/` tree. **Next:** 20.2 (top-level E2E example module), then FIX-117 → the 18.2 fix batch.

**Phase 18.2 (Manifest builder + GraphQL `.graphqls` description wiring) landed 2026-05-16.** New `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/` package (CLI-side; runtime `manifest/` lands in 18.7) exporting `BuildInput` struct + `Build(BuildInput) (*Document, error)`. Builder is read-only over the codegen pipeline's in-memory state (parser.Schema + RootConfig + []gen.TableContext + []gen.ViewContext + APIContext) — no re-parsing. `types.go` carries ~25 named types mirroring PRD §30.4 / §30.4.1 / §30.4.2 / §30.4.3 / §30.7 with JSON tags so 18.3's emitter marshals `*Document` directly. `builder.go` runs per-entity (kind / name / table / schema / file_prefix / files[] / pk{kind/struct/columns[]} / features / columns / relationships / methods / filter / sort / examples), with sub-builders split for cyclop-15 (`buildQueryMethods` / `buildMutationMethods` / `uniqueIndexFindMethods` / per-feature builders / per-extra-metadata helpers). `sql.go` captures per-method canonical SQL by calling the runtime `sql.Build*` helpers at codegen time with PK conditions (numbered placeholders) and `<filter>` / `<sort>` / `LIMIT $N` tokens for runtime-provided clauses — manifest and `_gen.go` runtime code agree by construction. New `sqlWalk` helper mirrors `sqlList` without LIMIT per PRD §9.4a (Stream is unbounded). GraphQL template side-effect: `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/schema.graphqls.tmpl` type-level `"""..."""` block now reads `Table.Comment` when non-empty, falling back to the existing placeholder otherwise (field-level + relationship-level already followed this pattern). `cmd/sqlgen/manifest/builder_test.go` carries 16 tests — every entity shape (single-PK, composite-PK, tenanted, soft-delete, cache, events, view, M2M), extended-metadata round-trip (comments, multi-column CHECK flatten, default_kind triage `literal`/`function`/`expression`, PK + unique indexes), `generation_config` both off-by-default and all-on toggle matrices, per-method `sql_bodies` for FindByID / FindByEmail / List / Count / Walk / Create / Update / HardDelete / SoftDelete / Upsert*, per-table opt-out via `tables.<name>.manifest.enabled: false`, top-level envelope ($schema URL / schema_version / generator / dialect / package / layout), conventions surface pinned against runtime (omittable constructors `Set`/`Omit`, methods `IsSet`/`IsZero`/`Get() (T, bool)`/`MustGet`; comparator families Bool/Enum/ID/JSON/JSONB/Number/Slice/String/Time), filter And/Or pinned to `[]*<T>Filter`. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_schema_test.go` adds `TestGraphQLSchema_DescriptionWiring` (with-comment + empty-comment fallback). **Verification:** `make check` clean across all 8 modules (cmd/sqlgen/manifest ~0.4s); `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (template change is byte-equivalent for example output since no example sets `Comment` on tables — graphql/tests ~34.6s, mysql/tests ~10.3s, postgres/tests ~5.2s, others <3s); test-integration not re-run for 18.2 — builder is read-only over in-memory state and SQL bodies route through `sql/`'s integration-tested builders; will run as part of the 18.10 closure sweep. **Carry-over for 18.3:** the 18.2 manifest builder produces a complete `*Document` ready for serialization — 18.3 only needs to wire `EmitJSON` with deterministic ordering, HTML-escaping disabled, and the per-entity layout splitter. **Carry-over needing /fix before 18.10:** (a) parser does not yet expose index `method` (btree/gin/gist/hash) or partial `WHERE`; builder hardcodes `method: "btree"` and never sets `where`, so the two "Partial UNIQUE" + "GIN/GIST" tests from 18.2's Tests Required are deferred (marked `[ ]` in phase doc) — file a FIX-NNN to extend `parser.Constraint` + dialect parsers before 18.10 closure. (b) Relationship kind disambiguation between `o2o` (parent side) and `m2o` (child side) uses GoType `*` prefix heuristic — works for current codegen output but fragile to refactors; worth a follow-up FIX if it bites. **Next:** 18.3 (JSON emission + JSON Schema v1.0.0 draft + `--manifest-timestamp` flag).

**Phase 18.1 (Manifest config + validation) landed 2026-05-16.** Pure config-side change. New `ManifestConfig` / `BreadcrumbsConfig` / `TableManifestConfig` structs + `JSONLayout` enum (`single` / `per_entity`) + `ManifestFormat{JSON,Markdown}` constants in `cmd/sqlgen/config/config.go`; `Manifest *ManifestConfig` wired onto `GenerationConfig` and `Manifest *TableManifestConfig` wired onto `TableConfig`. `applyManifestDefaults` fills the PRD §30.2 defaults (`formats: [json, markdown]`, `json_layout: single`, `markdown_dir: manifest`, `json_filename: manifest_gen.json`, `json_per_entity_dir: entities`, `include_examples: true`, `include_internal: false`, `embed_in_client: true`, all three `breadcrumbs.*: true`) only when `enabled` resolves true — no-op otherwise, and a complete absence of the `manifest:` block leaves `Generation.Manifest == nil` so existing example configs see zero behavior change. New `ResolveTableManifestEnabled` exposes the PRD §30.2 resolution order (table-level → global → false). `cmd/sqlgen/config/validate.go` adds `validateManifestConfig` (split into three sub-helpers `validateManifestEnabledFields` / `validateManifestDisabledFields` / `validateManifestPerTableOptIn` to stay under the cyclop-15 cap after the initial single-function landing tripped CC=25) wired into `ValidatePreParse`, covering all eight PRD §4.13 manifest rules — every error message cites the field name and `30.2 / 4.13`. `reservedManifestArtifactDirs` map seeded with `graph` (the only known PRD §8.3 artifact subdir today; future artifact subdirs add entries). New `cmd/sqlgen/config/manifest_test.go` carries 8 `TestValidatePreParse_Manifest_*` table-driven tests (one per validation rule; positive + negative branches assert both field name and PRD-section citation), 3 `TestLoadConfig_Manifest*` resolution tests (defaults applied end-to-end through YAML, omitted block leaves nil, per-table opt-out resolves correctly), and `TestResolveTableManifestEnabled` (5-case matrix). **Verification:** `make check` clean across all 8 modules under `-race` (cmd/sqlgen/config ~1.4s); `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules — graphql/tests ~36.6s under -race, mysql/tests ~10.3s, postgres/tests ~5.2s, others <3s; no example-config drift since the new field is optional. **Carry-over for 18.2:** none — 18.1 surface is complete and isolated; the manifest builder will consume `cfg.Generation.Manifest` and `ResolveTableManifestEnabled` directly. **Next:** 18.2 (Manifest builder package + GraphQL `.graphqls` description-wiring side-effect).

**Phase 18 (Manifest) design frozen + PRD §30 synced (2026-05-15).** `docs/design/MANIFEST.md` open-questions section emptied (0 open, 18 resolved) — gating signal for PRD sync per MANIFEST.md §9. PRD §30 "Manifest" landed end-to-end in `docs/PRD.md` (~600 LOC) covering: §30.1 overview + composition with cache / tenancy / events / soft-delete / views / GraphQL; §30.2 configuration (`generation.manifest` block with `enabled`, `formats`, `json_layout` single/per-entity, `markdown_dir`, `breadcrumbs.{claude_md,agents_md,package_doc}`, `embed_in_client`, per-table opt-out); §30.3 generated artifacts (file layout for both JSON layouts, stale-cleanup per §23.8, layered-GraphQL variant); §30.4 JSON schema (top-level fields + conventions block + entity shape + extended metadata); §30.5 versioning (independent semver on `schema_version`); §30.6 runtime-embedded manifest (new `manifest/` runtime package — peer to `database/`, `cache/` etc., stdlib-only — exporting ~25 types; three Client methods `Manifest()` / `ManifestEntity(table)` / `ManifestVersion()`; `//go:embed` + init-time parse); §30.7 extended schema metadata (`COMMENT ON TABLE` / `COMMENT ON COLUMN` capture, full `indexes[]` array, per-column `check` + `default_kind`, `features.events`, expanded `features.cache`); §30.8 CLI tooling (`sqlgen manifest validate` + `sqlgen manifest diff`). Cross-reference rows added to PRD §4.6 (Manifest subsection in GenerationConfig), §4.13 (8 new validation rules), §8.3 (Manifest row in Generated Artifacts), §23.1 (the two new CLI commands). `docs/tracker/IMPLEMENTATION_ORDER.md` Phase 18 entry added with 10 sub-items (18.1 config + validation, 18.2 builder + GraphQL `.graphqls` description-wiring side-effect bundled in, 18.3 JSON emission + schema, 18.4 markdown emission, 18.5 breadcrumbs + package doc-comment, 18.6 pipeline wiring + stale cleanup, 18.7 runtime-embedded manifest + Go types package, 18.8 CLI subcommands, 18.9 E2E across all dialects, 18.10 closure sweep + manifest schema v1.0.0 freeze). Sibling design doc `docs/design/MCP.md` carries the Phase 19 design (MCP server consuming the manifest); Phase 19 gated on Phase 18 closure + schema v1.0.0 freeze. **Next:** `/phase 18` to break into trackable sub-items, or finish 16.9 closure first if scheduling prefers strict phase order.

**16.8g (Migration breadcrumb + closure sweep) landed 2026-05-14 — 16.8 umbrella now Complete.** New `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/README.md` documents the FIX-064 migration path for consumers on the original 16.3-era layout: (1) delete `models/graph/{resolvers,field_options,connection_walker}_gen.go`; (2) add `Q *sqlgenresolver.Q` / `M *sqlgenresolver.M` fields to the gqlgen-scaffolded `Resolver` struct (sqlgen scaffolds greenfield, `mergeResolverScaffold` AST-edits an existing `resolver.go` on follow-schema); (3) initialize both at app boot with the same `*models.Client` (with `tests/main_test.go` cited as the reference harness); (4) re-run `sqlgen graphql gen` so the panic-stub rewriter swaps `r.Q.<Field>(...)` / `r.M.<Field>(...)` delegations into any newly-managed resolver methods gqlgen scaffolds as `panic("not implemented")`. README also pins gqlgen v0.17.90 as the validated version for the Shape A seed flow + documents the example layout / harness invocation. No code changes — pure docs + tracker. **Sweep results (2026-05-14):** `make check` clean across all 8 modules under `-race` (cli ~43s, gen ~9.5s, wrapper ~6.3s); `make lint-examples` + `make vet-examples` + `make test-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (graphql/tests leg ~30.2s — the 38 graphql tests from 16.8a–f under -race); `make test-integration` clean (cmd/sqlgen integration ~119s including `TestE2EGoldenFiles` over every example, cli integration ~64s); no flakes. **Pre-existing fmt drift surfaced and reverted during sweep:** the locally-installed `gofumpt v0.8.0` (invoked via `make fmt-examples` which `make check-examples` chains in front of `lint-examples` / `vet-examples` / `test-examples`) produces slightly different whitespace than the golangci-lint-bundled gofumpt that captured the committed goldens — nine files across `graphql` / `mysql` / `postgres` / `sqlite` example trees re-aligned, and one file (`expected/graph/shared_gen.resolvers.go`) collapsed two adjacent `type X struct{...}` declarations into a single `type (...)` block. Environmental, not 16.x regression; reverted before re-running the integration sweep, which then passed clean.

**16.8 umbrella closure delta (2026-05-05 → 2026-05-14).** All seven sub-items landed. **16.8a** (2026-05-06): example module skeleton + 8-table schema + `sqlgen.yml` baseline + `expected/` goldens; FIX-068 (M2M cross-PK-type fkToString) surfaced + resolved. **16.8b** (2026-05-08): `api.graphql` enabled + `gqlgen.yml` authored + `e2e_test.go` harness extension (`isGraphQLExample` + in-place generation for graphql examples) + full `expected/graph/` goldens (per-table `*_gen.graphqls`, sqlgen-seeded `*_gen.resolvers.go`, Shape A `sqlgenresolver/`, scalars / filter / sort / input translators, `api_envelopes_gen.go`); FIX-070 through FIX-082 (~13 codegen / wrapper / template fixes) surfaced + resolved. **16.8c** (2026-05-10): boot harness (postgres testcontainer + live gqlgen handler) + `curated_surface_test.go` (20 sub-tests over `User` / `Category` + 3 response-shape pin tests); **Phase 17 (Sub-Categorized Polymorphism) closed 2026-05-13.** All four sub-items complete: 17.1 (Config validation + relationship dedup key extension), 17.2 (Codegen — per-relationship loader emission), 17.3 (E2E example + cross-dialect integration tests), 17.4 (Phase closure sweep).

**17.4 closure sweep (landed 2026-05-13):** Full `make check` + `make check-examples` + `make test-integration` clean under `-race`. Check: 0 lint across 8 modules; `-short -race` unit tests green (cmd/sqlgen/cli ~39.7s, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5.5s, others <3s). Check-examples: 0 lint across 8 example modules; tests green (graphql/tests ~32.7s including curated-surface sub-categorized polymorphism + multi-column-filter cases, mysql/tests ~15.1s, postgres/tests ~6.2s, postgres_stdlib/tests ~3.2s, sqlite/tests ~2.1s, others <2s). Test-integration: full sweep (no `-short`) green; longest legs are cmd/sqlgen ~5.4 min and cmd/sqlgen/cli ~3.4 min (Docker-bound testcontainer suites); no flakes. FIX triage complete — the four 17.3 inline fixes (FIX-094 sub-categorized o2m client-ref duplication, FIX-095 filter-comparator mismatch on config-declared polymorphic FKs, FIX-096 column-enum → GraphQL-enum codegen including the slice path, FIX-097 sqlite `:memory:` shared-cache test-infra) were already filed and resolved in `docs/tracker/fixes.md` ahead of the sweep; FIX-098 (multi-column `filter:` identifier qualification — surfaced post-17.3 during PRD §13.7.1 multi-column hardening, resolved 2026-05-13) lands codegen-time identifier qualification per dialect via each dialect's native SQL parser (`parser/postgres/filter_qualifier.go`, `parser/mysql/filter_qualifier.go`, `parser/sqlite/filter_qualifier.go`) and extends the asset/documents example fixtures with `asset_document_links` (junction) + `PrimaryActiveDocument` (o2o) / `PhotoAttachments` (o2m) / `LinkedAttachments` (m2m) — all three exercising multi-column raw filters. **Surface delta summary (across Phase 17):** PRD §13.7 relaxed-dedup contract delivered end-to-end: per-relationship FieldOptions field emission, per-relationship loader generation with static `filter:` threading on O2O JOIN-ON and O2M / M2M target-query `conditions: []sql.Condition{sql.Raw(...)}` paths, multi-column filter auto-qualification (FIX-098), per-dialect example fixtures (postgres / mysql / sqlite / graphql) with sub-categorized `assets`/`documents` plus the FIX-098 multi-column extension, and GraphQL curated-surface support (enum + slice round-trip, FIX-096). Deferred items in PRD §13.7.4 — codegen-time `filter:` *contents* validation (column-existence), enum literal validation, reverse dispatch (§13.7.2) — remain out of scope per the phase scope rule. **Next:** no phase queued; the active In-Progress entries (Phase 13 13.11 lint rule gated on 12.12 infrastructure, Phase 16 16.8f–g) remain in their deferred / queued states.

**17.3 surface delta (landed 2026-05-12):** Added `assets` + `documents` table fixtures across all four example modules (`postgres/`, `mysql/`, `sqlite/`, `graphql/`) with three sub-categorized relationships on `assets` — `PrimaryDocument` (o2o), `Attachments` (o2m), `Invoices` (o2m) — each with a distinct `filter:` against the `entity_type` discriminator column. Postgres + mysql + graphql use enum-typed discriminators (`document_entity_type_enum` for the two PG-derived schemas, inline `ENUM('asset.primary', 'asset.attachment', 'asset.invoice', 'spv')` for mysql), sqlite uses TEXT (no native enum). Per-dialect integration tests (`cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite}/tests/sub_categorized_polymorphism_test.go`) seed 1 primary + 2 attachments + 1 invoice + 1 stranded `spv` row, query the parent with all three relationships set, assert subset membership and stranded-row exclusion. Curated-surface variant (`cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/tests/sub_categorized_polymorphism_test.go`) exercises the same shape through GraphQL mutations + a single nested-fields query. Goldens regenerated via `make update-golden-e2e`; diffs localized to the new tables apart from a cosmetic per-client field reorder introduced by FIX-094. **Four FIXes surfaced and resolved inline to ship 17.3:** **FIX-094** (sub-categorized o2m client-ref duplication — `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/table/client.go.tmpl` emitted one `<Target>Client` field per O2M/M2M relationship, so `Attachments` + `Invoices` both → `documents` compiled to `documentClient redeclared`; fix adds per-table-deduped `RelationshipTargetClients []string` separate from the cross-table-deduped `RelationshipOptionsDefs`). **FIX-095** (filter-comparator mismatch on config-declared polymorphic FKs — `documents.entity_id` with no SQL `REFERENCES` clause was typed `*comparator.String` while the loader emitted `&comparator.ID{In: ...}`; fix adds `applyConfigDeclaredFKs` pre-pass in `BuildTableContexts` that stamps synthetic `parser.FKReference` on every config-declared `fk:` column). **FIX-096** (column-enum → GraphQL-enum codegen, including the slice path — pre-existing gap surfaced the first time a PG enum column flowed through the GraphQL surface: `graphQLTypeForGoType` fell through to `String` so `documents.entity_type` emitted as `entityType: String!` and gqlgen generated a `documentResolver.EntityType` panic stub it couldn't bridge to `models.DocumentEntityTypeEnum`; the same lookup missed the PostgreSQL-array companion (`<Enum>Slice`) so a `document_entity_type_enum[]` column would hit the same panic-stub trap on output and a compile-time type-mismatch in the input translator. Fix lands the column-enum → GraphQL-enum projection end-to-end: new `APIEnumContext` / `APIEnumValue` on `APIContext.UsedEnums`; `BuildAPIContext` now accepts `[]EnumContext`; `graphQLTypeForGoType` resolves both bare-enum and slice-variant Go types to the enum's GraphQL name; `templates/api/shared.graphqls.tmpl` emits one `enum <Name> { ASSETPRIMARY ... }` per used enum (SCREAMING_SNAKE-cased Go constant suffix); `templates/enum.go.tmpl` gained `MarshalGQL` / `UnmarshalGQL` on BOTH the bare enum and its named-slice variant via duck typing (stdlib `io` only — no gqlgen import in the models package) that translate between the GraphQL identifier and the SQL literal on every read/write; `cli/graphql.go::buildMergeInput` binds `<EnumGraphQLName> → [<modelsPkg>.<Enum>, <modelsPkg>.<Enum>Slice]` so list-typed fields resolve to the named slice without resolver stubs; `buildAPIInputFields` detects named-slice casts (`models.<Enum>Slice(in.<Field>)`) and `funcInputCoerceDeref` applies them on the bare-nullable path; `gqlgenIsBareNullable` extended to `col.IsSlice || ...` so all slice columns flow uniformly through the no-deref branch. Verified end-to-end via the curated-surface test: mutation seeds with `[ASSETPRIMARY, ASSETATTACHMENT]` and the read query gets back the same identifiers). **FIX-097** (sqlite test infrastructure: `:memory:` is per-connection in `modernc.org/sqlite`, so multi-relationship errgroup loaders see empty schemas on parallel goroutines; fix switches `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/sqlite/tests/main_test.go` to `file::memory:?cache=shared`). **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (0 lint; graphql/tests ~27s, mysql/tests ~10s, postgres/tests ~5.5s, sqlite/tests ~2s, others <2s) — every new integration test green: `postgres/tests::TestSubCategorizedPolymorphism`, `mysql/tests::TestSubCategorizedPolymorphism`, `sqlite/tests::TestSubCategorizedPolymorphism`, `graphql/tests::TestCuratedSurface_SubCategorizedPolymorphism`. **Carry-over for 17.4:** none on functional surface — the four FIX entries above are documented in 17.3's Completion Record and should be triaged into `/fix` records during 17.4 closure. **Next:** 17.4 (Phase closure sweep — full integration sweep under `-race`, FIX triage, STATUS.md closure summary).

**17.2 surface delta (landed 2026-05-12):** Single template change in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/table/get.go.tmpl` — both the O2M loader (around line 390) and the M2M loader (around line 520) now conditionally emit `conditions: []sql.Condition{sql.Raw(<rel.Filter>)},` on the `Get<Target>Input` literal when `rel.Filter` is non-empty. The `conditions` slot was already wired through `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/shared/_input.tmpl:43` (`conditions []sql.Condition`) and `get.go.tmpl:205` (`conds = append(conds, input.conditions...)` → `sql.BuildSelect`), so the static filter AND's onto the WHERE clause alongside the consumer-supplied `fo.<Field>.Filter`. Empty `rel.Filter` skips the block entirely — every pre-17.2 example golden is byte-stable (`make check-examples` clean; no `make update-golden-e2e` needed). `sql.Raw` and the `sql` package are already imported by every parent client (used by `sql.BuildSelect` and the M2M junction query), no import wiring needed. This closes out the 17.1 carry-over note re: the dispatching predicate not reaching the SQL on O2M/M2M sub-categorized loaders. **GraphQL walker + schema generator required ZERO changes** — `api/field-options` (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/field_options.go.tmpl:48-58`) and `api/table-schema` (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/schema.graphqls.tmpl:12-15`) already iterate `Relationships` per-entry, so the 17.1 multi-entry contexts flow through into per-relationship `case "<field>":` emission + per-relationship GraphQL fields automatically. Walker-completeness lint (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker.go:34-44`) iterates by relationship `Name` (kept distinct per parent by 17.1's §4.8 validation), so it also required no change. **Tests:** `TestGetTemplate_subCategorizedO2MFilter` in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/get_test.go` (two O2M sub-categorized relationships on `asset` → two `if fo.<Field>` blocks, two distinct `conditions: []sql.Condition{sql.Raw(...)}` lines, 2× `g.Go`); `TestGetTemplate_subCategorizedM2MFilter` (M2M static filter threads through after junction); `TestGetTemplate_o2mNoStaticFilterNoConditions` (negative — empty `rel.Filter` MUST NOT emit `conditions:` so every existing example golden stays byte-stable, the load-bearing invariant for the change); `TestWalker_SubCategorizedRelationships` in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker_test.go` (one shared 3-relationship asset/documents fixture asserts walker case emission, target-FieldOptions descent, shared `DocumentRelationshipOptions` wrap for the O2M pair, walker-completeness lint pass, and per-relationship GraphQL schema field emission with correct cardinality suffixes — `Document` for o2o vs `[Document!]!` for o2m). **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5.7s including 4 new tests, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~44.9s). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (0 lint, all tests green; graphql/tests ~38.4s, mysql/tests ~12.7s, postgres/tests ~5.4s, sqlite/tests ~2.2s, others <2s — byte-stable since no example uses `filter:` on a relationship yet). **No FIXes surfaced.** **Carry-over for 17.3:** none — the 17.2 surface is complete end-to-end: config validation (17.1), per-table loader emission with static-filter threading (17.2), GraphQL walker per-relationship cases (17.2), GraphQL schema per-relationship fields (17.2), walker-completeness lint (17.2). 17.3 lands the example fixtures (assets + documents across postgres / mysql / sqlite / graphql), `make update-golden-e2e` regen, and per-dialect integration tests asserting each sub-categorized relationship returns its proper subset and stranded rows are silently excluded. **Next:** 17.3 (E2E example + cross-dialect integration tests — schema extension across all four examples, golden regen with localized diffs, per-dialect integration tests covering happy path + stranded-row exclusion + graphql curated-surface variant).

**17.1 surface delta (landed 2026-05-12):** Pure config-side validation addition in `cmd/sqlgen/config/validate.go` — new `validateRelationships` (wired into `ValidatePreParse`) enforces PRD §13.7.3's relationship dedup rules and §4.8's per-parent distinct-name requirement. The dedup key follows PRD §13.7.3 verbatim — `(target, fk, filter)` for o2o/o2m (single bucket — type is *not* part of the key, so o2o ↔ o2m collisions on identical `(target, fk, filter)` ARE flagged) and `(target, junction, junction_local_fk, junction_reference_fk, filter)` for m2m. Sentinel-prefixed structural distinction (`"x"` non-m2m / `"m"` m2m) prevents an empty-fk o2o/o2m entry from aliasing into the m2m bucket. `\x00` field separator (cannot appear in a SQL identifier or filter). New private `isM2M` helper mirrors `parseRelationshipType`'s `m2m`/`many_to_many` synonyms so validation and codegen agree on what counts as m2m for dedup-shape purposes — synonym variants of o2o/o2m (`one_to_one`↔`o2o`, etc.) are subsumed because `type` isn't keyed at all. `filter:` compared byte-equal per PRD §13.7.1 — no whitespace normalization. Diagnostic ordering is deterministic via a `sortedTableKeys` helper (Go map iteration is randomized). **Tests:** `TestValidatePreParse_Relationships_DedupRules` (11 table-driven cases — 7 spec-required + byte-equal whitespace distinction + one-empty-filter coexistence + FIX-093 mixed o2o/o2m collision + FIX-093 synonym-variant collision). `TestBuildTableContexts_SubCategorizedRelationships` in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/context_table_test.go` pins the codegen-context flow for three sub-categorized relationships on a minimal `asset` + `documents` fixture — name→filter map round-trip, Filter strings preserved verbatim, single `DocumentRelationshipOptions` emitted across all three sub-categorized fields. **No `cmd/sqlgen/gen/context_table.go` change required** — `buildRelationshipContexts` already appends per config entry; `RelationshipOptionsDef` already dedups by target struct name; the O2O alias generator (`generateAlias(rel.FieldName, usedAliases)`) keys on FieldName which 17.1's distinct-name validation guarantees. **One FIX surfaced and resolved inline: FIX-093** (the original implementation prepended `type` to the dedup key as a defensive measure; `/verify 17.1`'s reviewer pass flagged this as a deviation from PRD §13.7.3's explicit single-key-shape-for-o2o-and-o2m wording — dropped `type` from the key and locked the strict-PRD reading in via two new test cases). **Carry-over for 17.2:** the static `rel.Filter` is currently honored end-to-end *only on the O2O JOIN path* (`gen/templates/table/relationships.go.tmpl:6–10` AND's it into the join's `On` clause). The O2M/M2M relationship loaders in `gen/templates/table/get.go.tmpl:367–388` / `:494–517` merge `fo.<Field>.Filter` (FieldOptions-supplied at call time) onto the per-target `Filter` slice but do not consult the static config `rel.Filter`. With 17.1's validation relaxed, multi-entry O2M/M2M sub-categorization is structurally valid but the dispatching `filter:` predicate does not yet reach the generated SQL — 17.2's "per-relationship loader emission" tasks include threading `rel.Filter` into the O2M/M2M loader builders. Pre-existing test-file lint noise (4× `WriteString` inefficient-concat warnings in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/{filter,input,field_options}_test.go` plus an unused-`columnNameSet` in the file 17.1 touched) was cleared as a side-effect of the user-requested "fix any lint or vet issues" pass — three `wrap*ForCompile` test helpers switched their leading concatenations to `fmt.Fprintf` (byte-equivalent output, verified against existing goldens). **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/config ~1.4s incl. 11-case dedup-rule suite, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5.1s, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~39.9s). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (0 lint, graphql/tests ~36.5s, mysql ~12.4s, postgres ~5.6s — no regression from the validation addition; no example uses sub-categorized relationships yet, so no goldens to regenerate). **Next:** 17.2 (Codegen — per-relationship loader emission: confirm/extend O2M/M2M loader template to thread `rel.Filter` per the carry-over note, emit one `case "<field>":` per relationship in the GraphQL field-options walker, emit one selectable field per relationship in the GraphQL schema generator).

**Phase 16 (GraphQL API Generation) started 2026-04-29.** 16.1 (Schema generation + scalar marshaling), 16.2 (gqlgen wrapper subcommand), 16.3 (Resolver scaffolding — curated surface), 16.4 (Field selection translator — the central walker), 16.5 (Filter / sort / pagination translators), 16.6 (Error mapping + per-call options middleware), 16.7 (Multi-schema disambiguation + per-table API config), 16.8a (graphql E2E example skeleton + schema, pre-api.graphql), 16.8b (api.graphql block enabled + full goldens captured + e2e_test.go harness extension), 16.8c (boot harness `tests/main_test.go` + curated-surface coverage `tests/curated_surface_test.go` + response-shape pins), 16.8d (error codes + query count + scalars — `tests/error_codes_test.go` / `tests/query_count_test.go` / `tests/scalars_test.go`), and **16.8e (wrapper regressions — FIX-064 lock-in across `tests/wrapper_layered_test.go` / `tests/walker_lint_test.go` / `tests/wrapper_layout_test.go` / `tests/wrapper_rewriter_test.go` + shared `tests/wrapper_fixture_test.go` infrastructure)** complete.

**16.8e surface delta (landed 2026-05-12):** Four new test files under `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/tests/` plus a shared `wrapper_fixture_test.go` staging-and-subprocess helper, plus one wrapper bug fix surfaced by the new tests. **`wrapper_fixture_test.go`** — lazily builds the sqlgen binary in a per-process temp dir with the repo's workspace in scope (strips inherited `GOWORK=off` from `make test-examples` so the cmd/sqlgen module resolves its sibling parser/runtime imports through go.work), then exec's it with `GOWORK=off` so chained gqlgen subprocesses inherit module-mode resolution. `stageGraphQLExample(t)` copies the in-tree example to `t.TempDir()` minus `expected/` and `tests/` (those aren't part of the live consumer build graph), then rewrites the relative `replace github.com/teandresmith/sqlgen` directive to an absolute path so the staged copy resolves against the actual repo root. **`wrapper_layered_test.go`** — `TestWrapperLayered_PreservesConsumerEntries` pins PRD §26.5.6's "consumer owns gqlgen.yml end-to-end" guarantee: stages the example, pre-seeds `gqlgen.yml` with a custom `directives:` block plus a hand-bound `ConsumerOwnedScalar` model entry, swaps `api.graphql.gqlgen_bin` for a tiny POSIX shell stub that captures `--config <path>` to a known location and exits 0, runs `sqlgen graphql gen`, asserts SHA-equal pre/post on the consumer YAML AND that the captured merged temp config carries both consumer entries (`consumerOwnedDirective`, `ConsumerOwnedScalar`, `github.com/example/consumer/handbound.Type`) and sqlgen-emitted entries (per-table `UserConnection`/`ProductConnection`/`PageInfo` model bindings + `models/graph/*.graphqls` schema glob). **`walker_lint_test.go`** — `TestWalkerCompleteness_AllFieldsCovered` iterates every entity type declared on a `type <Name> { ... }` block in every checked-in `*_gen.graphqls` (filters envelope types — `<T>Connection`/`<T>Edge`/`<T>ListResult`/`PageInfo` — plus extension targets `Query`/`Mutation`) and asserts each declared field has a matching `case "<field>":` clause in the corresponding `<lowerType>FieldOptionsFromCollected` function inside `sqlgenresolver/field_options_gen.go`; covers all 8 example tables in one pass (Category / Event / Order / OrderItem / Product / Profile / User / UserCategory). `TestWalkerCompletenessLint_ErrorMessageFormat` greps `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker.go` for the §26.5.2-defined error-message format strings (`api: walker for table %q has no case for column %q`, `… relationship %q`, `PRD §26.5.2`) so accidental message reshapes surface symmetrically with the unit-test in `gen/api_walker_test.go`. Uses `github.com/gobuffalo/flect` (newly added as a direct require to the example's go.mod) for `Pascalize`/`Camelize`/`Underscore` so the test's casing matches what `cmd/sqlgen/gen/naming.go` uses for codegen — drift surfaces symmetrically. **`wrapper_layout_test.go`** — `TestWrapperLayout_CrossLayoutCompiles` stages the example, rewrites `gqlgen.yml`'s `resolver.layout` to the named variant, runs `sqlgen graphql gen` twice (real `go run github.com/99designs/gqlgen` subprocess), asserts `go build ./...` passes after each. Follow-schema variant locks in the FIX-064 spike's variant-7 outcome end-to-end. **`wrapper_rewriter_test.go`** — `TestWrapperRewriter_PanicStubFlipToDelegation` stages the example, scopes `orders` to `read_only` (deletes existing `order_gen.resolvers.go` so the seed step writes a read-only resolver from scratch), runs `sqlgen generate` to regenerate the schema + chain into the wrapper, asserts baseline absence of `SoftDeleteOrder` method; then flips the table's ops to `read_only + soft_delete: true + hard_delete: true + restore: true` (both delete ops must be on to produce the disambiguated `softDelete<Table>` mutation name — with only one enabled, the schema template emits unqualified `delete<Table>`), re-runs `sqlgen generate`, asserts the regenerated `order_gen.graphqls` declares `softDeleteOrder(`, the regenerated `order_gen.resolvers.go` carries `r.M.SoftDeleteOrder(...)` (the rewriter's delegation shape, not a `panic(...)` stub), and `go build ./...` succeeds — a regression in the rewriter's argument reconstruction would surface as a compile error. **Wrapper fix surfaced by the rewriter test:** `cmd/sqlgen/wrapper/seeds.go::rewriteOneFile` now calls `golang.org/x/tools/imports.Process` after `printer.Fprint` to drop unused imports. Panic-stub bodies reference `fmt.Errorf("not implemented: …")`, and rewriting every stub in a file leaves `fmt` imported but unreferenced — a Go compile error that the new `go build ./...` assertion surfaces. The fix is in-scope for 16.8e since the rewriter test naturally exposes it; `golang.org/x/tools/imports` is already a direct dep of `cmd/sqlgen` via `gen/format.go`. **Reconciliations (vs 16.8e spec text):** (1) **Walker lint via subprocess** — `buildAPITableContext` deterministically derives the walker from the same `TableContext.Columns` slice the lint checks against, so there's no fixture shape that triggers the divergence without modifying the codegen pipeline itself. The unit test in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker_test.go::TestWalker_CompletenessLint_NegativeTest` constructs in-memory contexts with a deliberately-missing column case and asserts the §26.5.2 error message; this E2E test pins the artifact-level invariant (every shipped walker IS complete against the shipped per-table schema) and re-pins the error-message format by reading the lint's source file. Unit test owns "lint fires with correct message"; E2E test owns "the lint's invariant holds in the shipped artifact". (2) **Single-file layout deferred** — gqlgen v0.17.90's resolvergen plugin wipes sqlgen's seeded `Resolver struct { Client; Q; M }` back to gqlgen-default `Resolver struct{}` on every run under single-file, breaking the `r.M.X(...)` delegations the rewriter emits (`*mutationResolver` has no embedded M field). The FIX-064 spike's variant 8 documented single-file as working but used a minimal fixture; the example's 8-table schema with full CRUD trips a code path the spike didn't. Closure deferred to a follow-on FIX that extends the post-gqlgen AST-merge (currently `mergeResolverScaffold` under follow-schema) to fire under single-file too. The follow-schema variant of the cross-layout test is the primary FIX-064 invariant and remains exercised end-to-end.
