(3) **Live handler in rewriter test** — the spec calls for "queries the newly-added softDelete<Table> mutation via the live GraphQL handler", but the example's main_test.go boots a handler bound to the in-tree models package and Go's runtime loader can't re-bind to a separately-staged copy at the same import path. Substituted three artifact-level checks: schema field exists, resolver body is the rewriter's delegation shape (not a panic stub), `go build ./...` succeeds. Runtime correctness of `r.M.SoftDelete<Table>` is covered by curated_surface_test.go's existing soft-delete tests against the in-tree fixture where the op is enabled from the outset. (4) **`sqlgen graphql gen` vs `sqlgen generate`** — the spec says the rewriter test runs `sqlgen graphql gen` twice. `api.operations` scoping affects schema generation, which happens in `sqlgen generate`. The rewriter test uses `sqlgen generate` so the operation-flip actually re-materializes the schema; FIX-078's chained `sqlgen generate` → `sqlgen graphql gen` flow exercises the same wrapper+rewriter path. (5) **`append_only` preset doesn't include soft-delete** — the spec says "flips to `append_only`"; looking at `config.go::ExpandPreset(PresetAppendOnly)`, append_only has `SoftDelete: false`, so the flip wouldn't actually gain `softDelete<Table>`. Substituted explicit individual toggles on top of read_only: `soft_delete: true + hard_delete: true + restore: true`. Both delete ops must be on to produce the disambiguated `softDelete<Table>` name — with only one, the schema template emits `delete<Table>` (gen/templates/api/schema.graphqls.tmpl §line 124-128). **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/wrapper ~15.7s including the new import-process path, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~44.3s, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~8.5s). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 example modules (graphql/tests ~26.6s — 4 new top-level test functions on top of the existing 16 + 25 curated; 0 lint issues across all 8 example modules). **Next:** 16.8f (cache-bypass + tenancy — `tests/cache_bypass_test.go` + `tests/tenancy_test.go` + `sqlgen.yml` cache/tenancy blocks).

**16.8d surface delta (landed 2026-05-12):** Three new files under `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/tests/`. **`error_codes_test.go`** — 5 top-level tests pinning every reachable PRD §26.5.5 sentinel through the live gqlgen handler: `TestErrorCodes_NotFound` (updateUser against a random UUID → `database.ErrNotFound` → `NOT_FOUND`); `TestErrorCodes_Conflict` (repeat createUser with the same UNIQUE email → `*ConstraintError{Type: ConstraintUnique}` → `CONFLICT`); `TestErrorCodes_BadReference` (createOrder with non-existent `user_id` UUID → `ConstraintForeignKey` → `BAD_REFERENCE`); `TestErrorCodes_InvalidInput_Check` (adds `CHECK (stock < 1000000)` on `products` via in-flight `ALTER TABLE` with `t.Cleanup` drop, mutates above threshold → `ConstraintCheck` → `INVALID_INPUT` with "check failed" message substring); `TestErrorCodes_InvalidInput_NotNull` (tightens `orders.notes` to NOT NULL via in-flight `ALTER TABLE`, createOrder omitting `notes` produces an INSERT without the column → postgres NOT NULL violation → `ConstraintNotNull` → `INVALID_INPUT` with "missing required"). Each case asserts both `extensions.code` (via the new `extensionsCode(gqlError) string` helper added to `main_test.go`) and the §26.5.5 message substring. **`query_count_test.go`** — `countingQuerier` mutex-guarded wrapper over `database.Querier` increments on every Exec/Query/QueryRow/Begin and delegates inward; `newCountingHandler(t)` boots a parallel gqlgen handler stack against the shared testcontainer with the counting Querier (seeding still goes through the standard `testServer` so warm-up queries aren't counted); `TestQueryCount_SingleTable` pins the §25.1 baseline (`user(id) { id email }` Get = exactly 1 query); `TestQueryCount_NestedRelationships` pins the corrected §25.1 formula (`user(id) { orders { orderItems { id } } categories { id } }` against a fully-seeded 3-level fixture = 1 base + 2 O2M + 2*1 M2M = 5 queries). **`scalars_test.go`** — `TestScalars` with 9 subtests covering every PRD §26.4.1 scalar category through the live handler: cat 2 Time (`users.created_at` via `time.Time`); cat 3 JSONMap (`users.metadata jsonb`), DateTime (`events.occurred_at` via `types.DateTime` override), NullDateTime (`events.processed_at` — set + null pair); cat 4 UUID (`users.id` round-trip), Decimal (`products.price`), NullUUID (`events.user_id` — set + null pair), NullDecimal (`events.adjustment` — set + null pair), JSON (`profiles.settings` via `json.RawMessage` override). Local `assertSameInstant` helper normalizes timestamptz comparisons via `time.Time.Equal()` rather than string-equal (the test runner's local TZ surfaces in the wire-format offset — the underlying instant is what matters; documented at file head). JSON objects with multiple keys are compared structurally via `cmp.Diff` rather than byte-equal — gqlgen re-serializes input variables via `json.Marshal`, which sorts map keys, so byte-equal-after-round-trip is only achievable on canonically-ordered inputs. **`main_test.go`** — added `extensionsCode(gqlError) string` helper for reading `extensions.code` off the gqlError envelope (empty-on-missing semantics let callers distinguish missing-code from typed mismatches); harness boot is unchanged. **PRD §25.1 amendment landed in same pass:** the original §25.1 formula (`1 + count(O2M) + count(M2M)`) and accompanying example diagram described M2M as a single `JOIN` query, but the actual runtime loader (`models_gen.go` per-table M2M block) loads M2M in **two batched queries** — one against the junction table to resolve the parent→target id mapping, then one against the target table via the target client's `GetMany` (composable per-table loader chain — soft-delete defaults, sort handling, recursive relationship loading all apply uniformly across every relationship kind). The §25.1 example diagram was rewritten to show the junction + entities split, the formula updated to `1 + count(O2M) + 2 * count(M2M)`, and a "Why two queries for M2M" paragraph documents the rationale. Corrected formula propagated to PRD §26.5 + §26.6 cross-references and the equivalent line in `docs/tracker/IMPLEMENTATION_ORDER.md` §16.8. **Reconciliations (vs 16.8d spec text):** (1) `INTERNAL` sentinel not pinned from the live handler — every reachable failure with this schema lands on a typed sentinel branch (NotFound / Conflict / BadReference / InvalidInput); INTERNAL is the structural catchall in the mapper and exercising it via the live handler would require deliberately corrupting runtime state in a way that doesn't model real consumer behavior. The catchall path is exercised at unit-test level via the mapper's own coverage (the resolver code referenced in 16.6). (2) `INVALID_INPUT` reached via in-flight `ALTER TABLE` adding a `CHECK` and tightening a column to NOT NULL — the input layer cannot send NULL through omittable for an already-NOT-NULL column (passing `null` collapses to Unset), and the example schema has no CHECK constraint; inverting these constraints under the test (with `t.Cleanup` restore) is the surgical way to reach the ConstraintCheck / ConstraintNotNull branches through the live handler. **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/cli ~39s, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~6.4s). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 examples (graphql/tests ~2.8s — 16 new tests + the existing curated-surface 25; testcontainer boot dominates the budget). **Next:** 16.8e (wrapper regressions — FIX-064 lock-in across `wrapper_layered_test.go` / `walker_lint_test.go` / `wrapper_layout_test.go` / `wrapper_rewriter_test.go`).

**16.8c surface delta (landed 2026-05-11):** New `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/tests/` package with two files. **`main_test.go`** boots a `postgres:16-alpine` testcontainer + `pgxpool.Pool` + `*models.Client`, seeds a `graph.Resolver{Client, Q: &sqlgenresolver.Q{Client}, M: &sqlgenresolver.M{Client}}`, hands it to `handler.NewDefaultServer(graph.NewExecutableSchema(graph.Config{Resolvers: resolver}))`, wraps with `sqlgenresolver.WithCallOptionsMiddleware`, and exposes via `httptest.NewServer`. Helpers: `gqlExec` (raw POST → `gqlResponse{Data, Errors}`), `gqlExecData` (no-errors-asserted decode into typed target), `truncateAll(t)` (TRUNCATE … RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE — see Reconciliation #1). **`curated_surface_test.go`** covers every PRD §26.5.1 curated-surface field on at least two tables (User uuid-PK + soft-delete + restore; Category int64-PK + plain delete): 22 sub-tests spanning Create / GetByPK (incl. null-on-miss pin) / Connection / List / CreateMany / Update / UpdateMany / Upsert / SoftDelete / Restore / HardDelete (User) and Create / GetByPK / Connection / List / CreateMany / Update / UpdateMany / Upsert / Delete (Category). Three response-shape pin tests (`TestResponseShape_PageInfo` / `_Edges` / `_ListResult`) cmp-diff JSON envelope keys against the PRD §26.4 spec values + assert wire types per field. **`go.mod` / `go.sum`** updated by `go mod tidy` to pull `github.com/google/go-cmp` + `testcontainers-go` + `testcontainers-go/modules/postgres` direct deps (16.8a deferred them; 16.8c lands them). **Two bugs surfaced + resolved during bring-up** (both in `docs/tracker/fixes.md` Resolved): **FIX-080** (walker) — `unwrapConnectionFields` template only descended `edges → node`, so `<Table>List { items { … } totalCount }` produced an empty FieldOptions and `Paginate` returned zero items via the `HasSelectedColumns()` early-return; resolved by extending the helper's switch to also unwrap `items`. **FIX-081** (single-by-PK) — `(q *Q) <T>(...)` piped every `Get` error including `ErrNotFound` through `mapErrorToGQL`, yielding `NOT_FOUND` GraphQL errors instead of `data.<table>: null` per PRD §26.5.1's explicit "returns null when missing"; resolved by adding `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) → return nil, nil` guard mirroring the existing soft-delete / restore branches in the same template. Both fixes landed in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/{connection_walker,resolvers}.go.tmpl`; `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker_test.go::TestWalker_ConnectionUnwrap` updated to the new switch shape; `make update-golden-e2e` regenerated the graphql example artefacts (`connection_walker_gen.go` body + per-table `sqlgenresolver/resolvers_gen.go` single-by-PK branches only — no other diffs). **Reconciliations (vs 16.8c spec text):** (1) No transactional per-test reset (spec line 690) — `TRUNCATE … RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE` exposed via `truncateAll(t)` and called explicitly by each top-level test instead. A transactional reset is impractical against a live gqlgen HTTP handler since each request takes its own connection from the pool, so the test transaction wouldn't be visible to the resolver. (2) `extensions.code` extractor deferred to 16.8d (the natural home — `error_codes_test.go` will assert sentinel codes per §26.5.5). The current `gqlError.Extensions` field is a `map[string]any`, so the 16.8d helper is a 5-line addition. (3) 22 subtests with explicit `t.Run` per surface kind rather than 22 top-level `Test*` functions — subtests share the once-per-table `truncateAll` cost and preserve declared order, so each kind operates against the state the prior kind left behind, matching how a real client would issue a sequence. **Verification:** `make check` clean (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/cli ~39s, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5.4s). `make check-examples` clean across all 8 examples (graphql/tests ~5.5s — postgres testcontainer + gqlgen HTTP handler + 25 tests under `-race`); `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql` clean. **Post-completion follow-up (2026-05-12):** `/verify 16.8c`'s reviewer subagent surfaced **FIX-082** — `unwrapConnectionFields`'s `case "items":` envelope branch was single-guarded (vs Relay's double-guard via inner `node` check), so a future consumer schema with a column or relationship literally named `items` (e.g. `Cart`'s O2M `items` relationship) would have a top-level `cart(id) { items { … } }` query misread as the offset envelope. Forward-compat trap — not a current-tree bug. Resolved by changing the helper signature to `(ctx context.Context, fields, connectionType, listResultType string)` and gating both envelope branches on `parentType` (derived from `graphql.GetFieldContext(ctx).Field.Definition.Type.Name()`) matching the table-specific `<T>Connection` / `<T>ListResult` literals passed in by each per-table entry point. New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker_test.go::TestWalker_EnvelopeGatingNamedItemsRelationship` drives a synthetic `carts` + O2M-`items` fixture pinning the contract at codegen time. Regenerated `make update-golden` + `make update-golden-e2e` — only graphql example's `connection_walker_gen.go` + `field_options_gen.go` diffed; `make check` + `make check-examples` still clean (graphql/tests live handler still passes all 22 curated-surface subtests + 3 response-shape pins, proving the gated unwrap still fires correctly for the genuine envelope path). **Next:** 16.8d (error codes + query count + scalars — `error_codes_test.go` / `query_count_test.go` / `scalars_test.go`) — unblocked.

**16.8b surface delta (landed 2026-05-08):** `api.graphql` block enabled in `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/sqlgen.yml` (`schema_dir: ./graph`, `resolver_dir: ./graph`, `package: graph`, `field_casing: camel_case`, `gqlgen_config: ./gqlgen.yml`, `gqlgen_bin: go run github.com/99designs/gqlgen`); plus a global `json → json.RawMessage` override completing the cat-4 JSON scalar exercise. New consumer-owned `gqlgen.yml` (paths align with `resolveAPIDir(output.dir=./models, schema_dir=./graph)` → `./models/graph/`). New `tools.go` (`//go:build tools` blank-import pinning `99designs/gqlgen` + `vektah/gqlparser/v2`) and `generate.go` (`//go:generate sqlgen graphql gen` directive). `go.mod` adds `99designs/gqlgen v0.17.90` + `vektah/gqlparser/v2 v2.5.33` to direct require. **Harness extension (`cmd/sqlgen/e2e_test.go`):** `isGraphQLExample` + inline `apiGraphQLEnabled` line-based parser detect `api.graphql.enabled` + a `gqlgen.yml`; graphql examples generate in-place at `ex.Path` rather than a temp dir (the chained `sqlgen graphql gen` gqlgen subprocess infers import paths from `go.mod`, and a `/tmp` dir would collide on the empty import path). `GOWORK=off` set unconditionally for every example so the chained `go run` subprocess works without colliding with the repo's `go.work`. `sqlgen generate` chains the wrapper invocation inline (FIX-078) so a single command produces both sqlgen-emitted and gqlgen-emitted artifacts. **Goldens captured under `expected/graph/`:** 8 per-table `*_gen.graphqls` + `shared_gen.graphqls` (9 scalars: DateTime / Decimal / JSON / JSONMap / NullDateTime / NullDecimal / NullUUID / Time / UUID), 8 per-schema `*_gen.resolvers.go` (post-gqlgen panic-stub-rewritten — every method delegates into `r.M.X(...)` / `r.Q.X(...)` confirming FIX-064 Shape A is firing), `sqlgenresolver/{resolvers,field_options,connection_walker,middleware,errors}_gen.go` (single `resolvers_gen.go` chosen over the spec's separate queries/mutations split per the spec's "final file split decided during implementation" allowance), `filter_translate_gen.go` + `comparator_translate_gen.go` + `input_translate_gen.go` + `sort_translate_gen.go`, `scalars_gen.go` (external marshalers for UUID / Decimal / JSON + paired NullUUID / NullDecimal per §26.4.1), gqlgen-emitted `generated_gen.go` + `model/models_gen.go`. Plus `expected/api_envelopes_gen.go` at output.dir root carrying the per-table generic-type aliases (`UserConnection = Connection[User]`, etc.) that gqlgen's `models:` merge binds to so the runtime envelope types flow through unchanged per §26.5.6's "Envelope binding" note. **Multiple FIX entries surfaced + resolved during 16.8b bring-up** (all in `docs/tracker/fixes.md` Resolved): FIX-070 through FIX-082 — covering callOptionsFromHTTP signature shape, graphql→Go ID type binding, output dir env var caching, gqlgen recognizing sqlgen-emitted resolvers, resolver wrapper subprocess go.sum drift, decimal increment recognition, PaginateInput/Result reshape, Connection model reshape, the `sqlgen generate` → `gqlgen generate` chain itself (FIX-078), Null-wrapper scalar bindings + paired Null variants (FIX-079 — split `builtInScalarRegistry` so each Null-wrapper Go type maps to its own `Null<Scalar>` entry, `mapColumnToGraphQL` skips the `!` suffix when resolved scalar is a Null variant), Connection/PaginateResult runtime → GraphQL binding, composite-key function generation for graph API, and generated files referencing gqlgen-generated types from the right package. **Reconciliations (vs 16.8b spec text):** (1) Single `sqlgenresolver/resolvers_gen.go` instead of separate `queries_gen.go`/`mutations_gen.go`. (2) Harness uses FIX-078's inline chain rather than separately invoking `sqlgen graphql gen`. (3) Graphql examples generate in-place at `ex.Path` rather than into `t.TempDir()` (chained gqlgen subprocess needs real `go.mod` context). (4) `GOWORK=off` applied unconditionally for every example, not just graphql. **Verification:** `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/cli ~39s, cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5.5s). `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql` passes (~6.4s). `GOWORK=off go build ./...` clean from example root. `GOWORK=off golangci-lint run ./...` 0 issues. gqlgen.yml SHA byte-equal pre/post `make update-golden-e2e`; idempotency confirmed across two consecutive runs. **Next:** 16.8c (boot harness `tests/main_test.go` + curated-surface coverage `tests/curated_surface_test.go` — booting a real testcontainer + live gqlgen HTTP handler + per-table coverage of every curated-surface mutation/query at least once).

**16.8a surface delta (landed 2026-05-05):** New `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/` example module — `go.mod` (pgx + uuid + decimal + sqlgen replace; gqlgen deps deferred to 16.8b), `sqlgen.yml` (global overrides on `uuid` / `numeric` / `json`; per-table `events.timestamptz → types.DateTime` override; soft-delete + `user_categories` cursor_keys), `schema.sql` (8 tables — categories with `BIGSERIAL` PK, others UUID; composite-PK M2M junction `user_categories`; soft-delete `deleted_at` on users / products / orders; every relationship variant — O2O via UNIQUE FK, M2O across mixed PK types, O2M reverse, M2M; one column per scalar registry category 1/3/4 — `uuid` / `numeric(12,2)` / `jsonb` / `json` / `timestamptz`). Goldens captured under `expected/` (8 files); `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql` passes against captured baseline; `make update-golden-e2e` byte-identical across consecutive runs (idempotency confirmed). **Two new FIX entries surfaced during bring-up:** **FIX-068 (resolved in 16.8a)** — M2M target loader at `gen/templates/table/get.go.tmpl:524` used `fkToString $pkField` (parent's PK FKConvert) when iterating target entities, failing on cross-PK-type M2M (the spec-required `users(uuid.UUID) ↔ categories(int64)` shape — emitted `t.ID.String()` against an `int64` target PK, compile error). Resolved by exporting `gotype.DeriveFKMethod`, adding `funcFKToStringByGoType` helper to drive conversion off the target's PK Go type (`RelationshipContext.FKGoType`), and switching the M2M target lookup to call it. Both directions now emit the correct conversion: target int64 → `fmt.Sprint(t.ID)`, target uuid.UUID → `t.ID.String()`. Existing `TestGetTemplate_goldenFile_withRelationships` fixture updated to set `FKGoType: "uuid.UUID"` on the M2M test relationship (production codegen populates this via `wireRelationshipFKMetadata`; unit fixtures must set it manually, mirroring FIX-059's `FKNullable` pattern). Regression coverage: `TestFuncFKToStringByGoType` (7 cases) + `TestFuncFKToStringByGoType_M2MCrossPKType` (anchors the cross-PK-type shape) in `cmd/sqlgen/gen/funcmap_test.go`. No E2E golden regeneration needed — every other example has matching parent/target M2M PK types, so the new helper produces byte-equal output. **FIX-069 (resolved 2026-05-05)** — O2M relationship loader emitted `r.<FK>.String()` on a nullable FK whose Go type was a Null-wrapped struct (e.g., `uuid.NullUUID`); compile error since `uuid.NullUUID` has no `String()` method. Resolved by adding `gotype.ScalarExtraction` (resolver-aware guard / unwrap descriptor) + `RelationshipContext.FKColumnGoType` plumbing, four new `config.NullableVariant` YAML fields (`underlying_field` / `valid_field` / `valid_method` / `valid_invert`) so user-declared wrappers participate, and `fkLoadGuard` / `fkLoadKey` funcmap helpers. The 16.8a `events.user_id NOT NULL` workaround was reverted; the graphql E2E example now exercises the fix path (`if !(r.UserID.Valid) { continue }; key := r.UserID.UUID.String()`). **Three reconciliations vs spec text** (documented in phase-16.md 16.8a Reconciliations section): (1) `json` cat-4 override implemented as global `overrides.types.json` instead of column-level (column_map.{type,import} not wired into the resolver — only `column_map.<col>.description` is consumed; per-SQL-type override produces the equivalent net effect since `json` and `jsonb` are distinct mapping-table entries). (2) `timestamptz` cat-3 DateTime override implemented per-table on a dedicated `events` table (smallest reachable lever overriding exactly one column without affecting other timestamp columns). (3) `events.user_id` was temporarily made `NOT NULL` in 16.8a as a FIX-069 workaround; reverted to nullable after FIX-069 was resolved so the example exercises the new guard-+-unwrap path. `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5s, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~37s) and `TestE2EGoldenFiles/graphql` pass cleanly. `GOWORK=off go build ./models/...` clean from the example dir; `GOWORK=off golangci-lint run` 0 issues. **Next:** 16.8b (enable `api.graphql` block + capture goldens + extend `e2e_test.go` to invoke `sqlgen graphql gen`) — unblocked. FIX-066 + FIX-067 already resolved.

**16.7 surface delta (landed 2026-05-01):** Sub-item closed by adding test coverage for the per-table API config + multi-schema disambiguation surface — no codegen changes were needed, since `tableAPIEnabled` (in `context_api.go::BuildAPIContext`) already short-circuits per-table API emission and `gen.StructName` (reused per the 16.1 forward-compat task) already disambiguates same-named tables across schemas. Two new test files: `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_multischema_test.go` (1 test, 24 assertions across `audit.users` + `public.users`) and `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_per_table_test.go` (5 tests covering `api.enabled:false` exclusion + the four operations presets — `read_only` / `no_delete` / `no_hard_delete` / `append_only`). **Reconciliation (vs phase-16.md acceptance text):** the spec text said "audit.users and public.users coexist as `AuditUser` and `User`" — but the actual landed `gen.StructName` symmetric-prefix behaviour produces `AuditUser` AND `PublicUser` (the postgres E2E example's `expected/tablenames_gen.go` already pins both). The test asserts the actual behaviour with an inline header comment — same reconciliation pattern 16.6 used. Tenancy mapping (`tenancy.ErrMissing` → `UNAUTHENTICATED`, `ErrMismatch` → `FORBIDDEN`) was pinned in 16.6's unit tests, so no separate codegen test was added here. `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5s, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~37s) passes cleanly. **Next:** 16.8 (E2E example module — `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/graphql/` with full GraphQL stack: gqlgen subprocess + counting Querier wrapper + cache-bypass + tenancy + every curated-surface mutation/query at least once).

**16.6 surface delta (landed 2026-05-01):** Two new templates emit the error-mapping + per-call-options middleware required by PRD §26.5.5 / §26.11. New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/errors.go.tmpl` emits `graph/errors_gen.go` with `mapErrorToGQL(err error) error` covering the full sentinel → `extensions.code` table — `database.ErrNotFound` / `tenancy.ErrMissing` / `tenancy.ErrMismatch` matched via `errors.Is`, and the four constraint-violation sentinels (`ErrUniqueViolation` / `ErrForeignKeyViolation` / `ErrCheckViolation` / `ErrNotNullViolation`) dispatched through a single `errors.As(*database.ConstraintError)` extraction + `switch ce.Type` (the runtime exposes constraint violations via the structured `ConstraintError`, not as standalone sentinels — `errors.As` walks the wrap chain so wrapping is still absorbed). Fall-through produces `{Message: err.Error(), code: INTERNAL}`. New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/middleware.go.tmpl` emits `graph/middleware_gen.go` with `WithCallOptionsMiddleware(next http.Handler) http.Handler` extracting `Cache-Control: no-cache` / `X-Skip-Events: true` / `X-Skip-Hooks: true` headers and stashing a non-generic `httpCallOptions{SkipCache, SkipEvents, SkipHooks bool}` struct under an unexported `callOptionsKey` type, plus `callOptionsFromHTTP[FO any](ctx) []func(*<pkg>.CallOptions[FO])` that reads the flags back and projects each truthy flag into a typed setter. The PRD's `[]func(*CallOptions)` example is illustrative — the actual generated `CallOptions[FO]` is generic, so the read side must be generic to type-match the resolver's per-table `CallOptions[<Table>FieldOptions]` parameter. Setters are appended in PRD §26.11's documented header order (Cache-Control → X-Skip-Events → X-Skip-Hooks). `SkipHooks ⇒ SkipCache + SkipEvents` is enforced by the runtime's `resolveCallOptions` (see `shared_types_gen.go`), so the middleware records only the explicit flag. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/orchestrate.go::generateAPI` now calls `generateAPISupportFiles` (extracted helper to keep `generateAPI` under the cyclop limit) which emits the two files: errors is unconditional (no consumer-models dep), middleware gates on `apiCtx.ModelsImportPath` resolving (it references `<pkg>.CallOptions[FO]`) and uses the explicit aliased-import pattern from `buildResolverFile` / `buildAPIWalkerFile` / `buildAPIFilterFile` so the consumer's models package is pinned to `cfg.Output.Package` even when the import path's last segment differs. **Reconciliations (vs PRD §26.5.5 / §26.11 as originally drafted):** (1) PRD §26.5.5's listed sentinels (`sqlgen.ErrUniqueViolation`, etc.) don't exist as standalone runtime values — the runtime exposes constraint violations through `*database.ConstraintError` with a `Type` field. The generated mapper dispatches via `errors.As` + `switch ce.Type` against `database.ConstraintUnique` / `ConstraintForeignKey` / `ConstraintCheck` / `ConstraintNotNull`. No runtime additions needed — `errors.As` absorbs wrapping just like `errors.Is`. (2) PRD §26.11's `func callOptionsFromRequest(r *http.Request) []func(*CallOptions)` example is non-generic; the actual generated `CallOptions[FO]` is generic, so the implementation is `func callOptionsFromHTTP[FO any](ctx context.Context) []func(*<pkg>.CallOptions[FO])` — context-keyed pull (matches the resolver template's existing `for _, f := range callOptionsFromHTTP(ctx) { f(o) }` shape) with a generic projection on read. (3) Stash is a flag struct (`httpCallOptions`), not a `[]func(*CallOptions)` slice — the slice can't survive `context.Value` round-tripping with the right element type for an unknown future FO; projecting from a flag struct on read produces the same observable behaviour at the resolver-call site. **Tests (2 new files, 8 new tests):** `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_errors_test.go` (3 tests covering the full PRD §26.5.5 sentinel mapping table-driven, the `errors.Is`/`errors.As` shape with negative assertion forbidding `err == database.…` direct equality, and the INTERNAL fall-through with nil-input short-circuit), `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_middleware_test.go` (5 tests covering each header dispatch path independently, the all-three-headers declared setter order via positional index assertion, and the round-trip stash/retrieve invariant including the unexported callOptionsKey type + httpCallOptions struct + generic signature). `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint after extracting `generateAPISupportFiles` to fix a cyclomatic-complexity overflow on `generateAPI`, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5s, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~44s) passes cleanly. No example module enables API generation yet, so the new template references don't break example builds — full compile is sequenced for 16.8 E2E. **Next:** 16.7 (Multi-schema disambiguation + per-table API config — PRD §26.4 / §26.5.5 / §26.10).

**16.5 surface delta (landed 2026-04-30, IncludeDeleted dropped 2026-05-01):** Three new templates emit the comparator → filter → sort translation surface required by PRD §26.5.3. New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/comparator_translate.go.tmpl` emits `graph/comparator_translate_gen.go` with one translator per comparator-family variant referenced by any table — `translateStringComparator`, `translateIDComparator`, `translateBooleanComparator`, `translateTimeComparator` plus `translateNumericComparator<T>` per unique numeric Go T (e.g. `Int32`, `Int64`, `Float64`); each family has an optional `translateNullable<X>Comparator` wrapper that delegates to the base translator and lifts the result into `comparator.Nullable<X>` so filters on nullable columns receive the correct model-side type. Numeric translators cast from the GraphQL `Float`-bound `*float64` operator fields into the underlying Go T (`v := int32(*in.Eq); out.Eq = &v`). New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/filter_translate.go.tmpl` emits per-table `translate<Table>Filter(*<Table>FilterInput) *<pkg>.<Table>Filter` — entry-point nil-guard, per-non-PK-column field-level dispatch via the matching family translator, and recursive `translate<Table>Filter(sub)` calls inside `for _, sub := range in.And/Or { ... }` loops with defensive nil-skip on sub-entries. **No `includeDeleted` flag** — the soft-delete column itself is a regular comparator field on the filter input (e.g. `deletedAt: TimeComparator`), and the runtime's existing `c.excludeDeleted && input.Filter.<DeletedField> == nil` guard in `template/table/get.go.tmpl:184` already short-circuits the auto-injected "is not deleted" condition whenever the caller passes any non-nil deletedAt comparator. To include soft-deleted rows, GraphQL callers send `deletedAt: {}` (an empty comparator that adds no SQL but defeats the auto-inject). New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/sort_translate.go.tmpl` emits a single shared `directionToSortDirection(SortDirection) sql.SortDirection` helper plus per-table `translate<Table>Sort([]*<Table>SortInput) []sql.Sort` and `<table>SortFieldToColumn(<Table>SortField) string` switch — switch cases match on the raw enum string (`case "CREATED_AT":` → `return "created_at"`) so the generator does not need to mirror gqlgen's PascalCase Go-const naming for acronym-bearing column names. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/context_api.go` extended with `APIComparatorTranslator` (FuncName / Family / Nullable / NumericT / GoReturnType / WrapperStructName / EmbeddedFieldName / BaseFuncName / BaseStructName), `APIFilterField` (GoFieldName / ModelFieldName / TranslatorFunc), and `APISortField` (EnumValue / SQLColumn) types. New `comparatorTranslatorVariant` / `buildAPIFilterFields` / `buildAPISortFields` builder helpers. `BuildAPIContext` accumulates the unique translator set into `APIContext.ComparatorTranslators` (sorted by func name). `cmd/sqlgen/gen/orchestrate.go::generateAPITranslatorFiles` orchestrates emission — comparator translators emit when at least one is registered (plus a conditional `time` import when the Time family is referenced); sort translators emit when at least one table has columns; filter translators gate on `ModelsImportPath` (consumer's models-package import) being resolvable. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/schema.graphqls.tmpl` updated to drop the `includeDeleted: Boolean` field from per-table filter inputs. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/shared/_filter.tmpl` and `ViewContext` are unchanged — the model layer is unmodified by 16.5. **PRD §26.5.3 updated** to drop `includeDeleted` from the example schema and translator code, document the column-level mechanism for soft-delete inclusion, correct the sort return type to `[]sql.Sort`, and correct the list-pagination type to `<pkg>.PaginateInput[F]`. **Reconciliations (vs PRD §26.5.3 as originally drafted):** (1) PRD named the sort return type `[]sort.Order`; the runtime type is `sql.Sort`, so the translator emits `[]sql.Sort` directly. PRD now corrected. (2) Phase doc referenced `models.ListInput[F]` for list pagination; the actually-emitted type is `models.PaginateInput[F]` (the existing client-side type). PRD now corrected. (3) Numeric T conversion: GraphQL `Float` binds to Go `float64`; per-T translators cast via `int32(*in.Eq)`. Decimal columns are not yet covered — out of scope for 16.5. (4) PRD §26.5.3 originally sketched `out.IncludeDeleted = *in.IncludeDeleted` as the pass-through. Implementation drops the flag entirely — the soft-delete column comparator field already drives the runtime's bypass logic, making a dedicated GraphQL flag redundant. PRD now corrected. (5) Switch cases in `<table>SortFieldToColumn` compare on the raw enum string rather than gqlgen-generated Go const names. **Tests (4 new files, 11 new tests):** `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_comparator_test.go` (4 tests covering String / Numeric / Time / Boolean per dialect — same fixture sweep across postgres / mysql / sqlite since the translator emit is dialect-independent — plus a dedicated `IDOnlyWhenForeignKey` test confirming IDComparator emits only when a non-PK FK ID column exists), `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_filter_test.go` (3 tests pinning recursive And/Or shape, deletedAt is dispatched as a regular nullable Time field — with negative assertions forbidding `in.IncludeDeleted` / `out.IncludeDeleted` / `&comparator.NullableTime{}` symbols, and a no-soft-delete shape check), `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_sort_test.go` (2 tests pinning the column-lookup switch every-case + the no-tables-no-emission edge case), `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_pagination_test.go` (2 tests pinning Connection / List arg pass-through and the absence of `translate*Pagination` symbols). `filter_test.go::TestFilterTemplate_softDeleteAsRegularComparator` retains its original "no IncludeDeleted on the model" assertion (the model layer is unchanged). `testdata/golden/filter_products_gen.go` matches the pre-16.5 baseline. `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen ~5s, cmd/sqlgen/cli ~37s) passes cleanly. No example module enables API generation yet, so the new template references don't break example builds — full compile is sequenced for 16.8 E2E. **Next:** 16.6 (Error mapping + middleware per PRD §26.5.5 / §26.11 — `mapErrorToGQL(err) error` covering the full sentinel → `extensions.code` table + `callOptionsFromHTTP(ctx) []func(*CallOptions)` per-call-options middleware).

**16.4 surface delta (landed 2026-04-30):** New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/field_options.go.tmpl` emits the per-table `<table>FieldOptionsFromContext` (entry point) + `<table>FieldOptionsFromCollected` (recursive walker) per PRD §26.5.2. Entry point pulls `opCtx := graphql.GetOperationContext(ctx)`, then routes through the shared `unwrapConnectionFields` helper so root Connection-shaped queries (`products { edges { node { ... } } }`) descend `edges` → `node` before the per-table switch sees real field selections. Each column emits `case "<graphqlName>": fo.<GoFieldName> = true`; each relationship recurses into the target table's walker — O2O assigns the bare `*<Target>FieldOptions` (matching `_field_options.tmpl`'s `isO2O` branch), O2M / M2M wrap into `&<pkg>.<Target>RelationshipOptions{FieldOptions: ...}` (matching the non-O2O branch). New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/connection_walker.go.tmpl` emits a single shared `unwrapConnectionFields(opCtx, fields) []graphql.CollectedField` helper to `graph/connection_walker_gen.go` — descends one level of the conventional Relay shape and falls through to the input unchanged when no `edges` field is present (Get / List queries already select columns directly). New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker.go::ValidateAPIWalkerCompleteness` runs at codegen time inside `BuildAPIContext` immediately after each `buildAPITableContext` returns — compares the source `TableContext.Columns` / `.Relationships` against the just-built `APITableContext.Fields` / `.Relationships` and surfaces `api: walker for table %q has no case for column/relationship %q` if any are missing. The lint runs even when the orchestrator's resolver-emit guard (`apiCtx.ModelsImportPath != ""`) trips, so misconfiguration produces a clear error rather than a silent skip. `cmd/sqlgen/gen/orchestrate.go` extracts the modules-package-dependent file emission into `generateAPIResolverFiles(...)` (now emitting three files: `resolvers_gen.go`, `field_options_gen.go`, `connection_walker_gen.go`) so the cyclomatic-complexity budget on `generateAPI` stays within the linter limit. `buildAPIWalkerFile` mirrors `buildResolverFile`'s explicit aliased-import-block pattern (consumer's models package pinned to `cfg.Output.Package` even when the import path's last segment differs); `FormatOnly` skips goimports' module-cache scan since the consumer's models package may not exist during a fresh `sqlgen gen` run. The connection walker file uses standard `renderAndWrite` since it only references `github.com/99designs/gqlgen/graphql`. `APIFieldContext` + `APIRelationshipContext` gained `GoFieldName` (populated from `ColumnContext.FieldName` / `RelationshipContext.FieldName`) so the walker template emits `fo.<Name> = ...` directly without re-PascalCasing in template land. **Three reconciliations documented inline:** (1) Real `graphql.CollectFields` requires `*OperationContext` — PRD §26.5.2 illustrates a 2-arg call (`graphql.CollectFields(f.Selections, nil)`) but the actual gqlgen API is 3-arg (`CollectFields(opCtx, selSet, satisfies)`). The walker entry point pulls `opCtx` once via `GetOperationContext(ctx)` and threads it through the recursive walker signature (`FieldOptionsFromCollected(opCtx *graphql.OperationContext, fields []graphql.CollectedField)`). Visible in `TestWalker_FragmentAndDirectiveCoverage`. (2) Helper named `unwrapConnectionFields`, not `unwrapConnectionAndWalk` per phase doc — the impl just returns the unwrapped field slice; the per-table entry point owns the recursive walker call. Same outcome (one shared impl emitted once), cleaner separation. (3) Runtime `FieldOptions`-tree byte-equality testing deferred to 16.8 — would require either compiling generated walker source via `plugin.Open` or re-implementing the walker in tests (tautological). 16.4 tests pin codegen-layer invariants only; 16.8 E2E example covers runtime contract end-to-end. **Tests (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_walker_test.go`):** 6 tests using a `walkerFixtureSchema` covering every relationship variant (O2O `company`, O2M `reviews`, M2M `tags` via `product_tags`) plus scalar columns (PK + non-null + nullable + timestamp) — `TestWalker_PerTableEmissionShape` (entry-point + recursive-walker shape, every column case, every relationship recursion); `TestWalker_DeeplyNestedFixtureQuery` (each relationship variant pinned at codegen layer; runtime depth covered by 16.8 E2E); `TestWalker_FragmentAndDirectiveCoverage` (anchors on `CollectFieldsCtx`/`GetOperationContext`/`CollectFields(opCtx, ...)` template usage — gqlgen's CollectFields handles fragments / `@skip` / `@include` / aliases by construction); `TestWalker_CompletenessLint_NegativeTest` (function-call test on `ValidateAPIWalkerCompleteness` with deliberately-mismatched contexts); `TestWalker_ConnectionUnwrap` (helper shape + per-table routing through it); `TestWalker_UnselectedRelationshipReturnsNil` (zero-value initialization + each relationship assignment count = 1, never eager). `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen 5.0s) passes cleanly. No example module enables API generation yet, so the new resolver-template references (`<table>FieldOptionsFromContext`, `unwrapConnectionFields`) don't break example builds — full compile is sequenced for 16.8 E2E. **Next:** 16.5 (Filter / sort / pagination translators per PRD §26.5.3 — per-comparator-family translators in `graph/comparator_translate_gen.go` + per-table `translate<Table>Filter` driving them + sort translator + pagination pass-through).

**16.3 surface delta (landed 2026-04-30):** New `cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/resolvers.go.tmpl` emits the §26.5.1 curated GraphQL resolver surface — `Resolver` root struct (wraps the configured unified client), `queryResolver` / `mutationResolver` types satisfying gqlgen's `Query()` / `Mutation()` interface methods, plus per-table query resolvers (`<table>` → unified `Get`, `<table>s` → unified `Connection`, `<table>List` → unified `Paginate` returning `*PaginateResult[T]`) and per-table mutation resolvers (`Create<Table>` / `Create<Table>s` / `Update<Table>` / `Update<Table>s` / `Upsert<Table>` plus the §26.5.1 delete-naming variants — bare `Delete<Table>` for hard-only / soft-only modes; explicit `HardDelete<Table>` + `SoftDelete<Table>` for both-mode; `Restore<Table>` whenever soft delete is enabled). Inline `translateCreate<Table>Input` / `translateUpdate<Table>Input` helpers handle the gqlgen-generated input → `*models.<Op><Table>Input` (omittable) translation; `_inc`/`_dec` paired-input dispatch follows the §26.5.4 order — `_set` (gated by `hasUpdate<Table>SetFields(input)`) → per-numeric-column `Increment(+amt)` → per-numeric-column `Increment(-amt)` (PRD §9.8.9a — only `Increment` is on the unified client; `Decrement` = negated amount on the same hook chain) → final `Get(id, FieldOptions)` re-fetch so the mutation result reflects the post-dispatch state. Curated surface enforced — no `Exists` / `Count` / `GetMany` / `Find` / `Increment` / `Decrement` root resolvers per §26.5.1's excluded-methods table. **Codegen-time conflict guard** (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/context_api.go::validateIncDecNamespace`) rejects schemas where a numeric non-PK column `c` has a sibling column literally named `c_inc` or `c_dec` — the auto-emitted `_inc`/`_dec` paired fields would otherwise duplicate the sibling's bare field; error names both colliding columns. **Runtime conflict guard** (`invalidInputError` helper + per-numeric-column checks at resolver entry) rejects `_set + _inc` on the same column AND `_inc + _dec` on the same column with `gqlerror.Error{Extensions: {"code": "INVALID_INPUT"}}` per §26.5.4. **Module-path resolution** (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/module.go` — new) provides public `ReadModulePath(dir)` + `JoinModulePath(modulePath, dir)` so `generateAPI` can populate `APIContext.ModelsImportPath` / `ModelsPackage` / `ClientName` from the consumer's `go.mod` + `cfg.Output`. The resolvers_gen.go file uses an explicit aliased import block (consumer's models package pinned to `cfg.Output.Package` even when the import path's last segment differs) and `FormatOnly` (no goimports cache scan) since the consumer's models package may not exist in the module cache during a fresh `sqlgen gen` run; imports are scoped per feature flag (`context` for any resolver method, `errors` for delete branches, `gqlerror` always — the `invalidInputError` helper ships unconditionally, `omittable` for any create/update mutation, the consumer's models package always). **Three reconciliations documented inline at phase-16.md:** (1) `Decrement` is not on the unified client — resolver emits `Increment(ctx, id, IncrementInput{Amount: -int(*input.<Col>Dec)})` per §9.8.9a (no PRD edit; §26.5.4 already references §9.8.9a as source-of-truth). (2) List resolver returns `*PaginateResult[T]`, not the schema-side `<Table>ListResult` envelope — runtime envelope conversion is sequenced for 16.5 (the filter / sort / pagination translation phase). (3) `flect.Pascalize("stock_dec")` returns `StockDEC` (acronym auto-detection) — would not match gqlgen's golint-style `StockDec` output. The fix sidesteps flect for the suffix: `APIUpdateOp.IncGoField = toPascalCase(col.Name) + "Inc"` and `DecGoField = toPascalCase(col.Name) + "Dec"`. **Tests (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_resolvers_test.go`):** 11 test cases — `TestResolvers_CuratedSurface_NoExcludedMethods` (excluded-symbol negative test); `TestResolvers_OperationsPresetGating` (table-driven over `read_only` / `no_delete` / `append_only`); `TestResolvers_SoftDeleteRequiresSoftDeleteColumn`; `TestResolvers_UpsertRequiresPKOrUnique`; the three delete-naming-rule variants (`_HardOnly` / `_SoftOnly` / `_Both`); `TestResolvers_MutationResultNullability`; `TestResolvers_IncDec_DispatchOrder` (anchors on `r.Client.Products().Update(ctx, id, setIn` / `Amount: int(*input.StockInc)` / `Amount: -int(*input.StockDec)`); `TestResolvers_IncDec_RuntimeConflictGuards`; `TestResolvers_IncDec_ConflictRejectedAtCodegen`. `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/gen 4.6s) and `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all E2E `-race` tests green) pass cleanly. No example module enables API generation yet, so the partial 16.3 → 16.4 → 16.5 → 16.6 codegen output (resolver template references not-yet-implemented helpers like `productFieldOptionsFromContext` / `translateProductFilter` / `mapErrorToGQL` / `callOptionsFromHTTP`) does not break example builds — full compile is sequenced for 16.8 E2E.

**16.2 surface delta (landed 2026-04-30):** New `cmd/sqlgen/wrapper/` package — `Init(path)` no-op-on-existing scaffold of `gqlgen.yml`; `Merge(consumerYAML, MergeInput) ([]byte, error)` operates on a `yaml.v3` AST with consumer-wins precedence on key collision (consumer's `directives:` / hand-bound `models:` / `scalars:` survive every wrapper invocation); `Gen(ctx, GenOptions) error` reads consumer config → calls `Merge` → writes a `os.CreateTemp` temp file → invokes `gqlgen_bin` via `exec.CommandContext` → `defer os.Remove` cleanup on both success and subprocess failure paths. `splitBin` whitespace-splits the default `"go run github.com/99designs/gqlgen"` into argv. New `cmd/sqlgen/cli/graphql.go` wires `sqlgen graphql init` and `sqlgen graphql gen` subcommands; `gen` runs the standard `loadAndValidate` pipeline → new public `gen.BuildTableContextsFromSchema` (wraps resolver/collisions/tenancy/relationship-options-dedup without writing files) → `gen.BuildAPIContext` → `buildMergeInput` (per-table model = `<module>/<output_dir>.<StructName>`; per-scalar model = `<GoImport>.<TypeName>` for non-builtin scalars; `cfg.API.GraphQL.Scalars` config entries appended last) → `wrapper.Gen`. **Module-boundary invariant:** `cmd/sqlgen` does NOT import gqlgen — verified by `go list -m all 2>&1 | grep -i gqlgen` returning empty plus `wrapper_test.go::TestNoGqlgenDependency` source-grep guard; the consumer-controlled `gqlgen_bin` is launched as a subprocess with a `//nolint:gosec G204` annotation per PRD §26.5.6. **Tests (`cmd/sqlgen/wrapper/`):** `wrapper_test.go::TestMain` doubles as a stub gqlgen binary (`SQLGEN_WRAPPER_TEST_HELPER=succeed/fail` env-var dispatch) so success/failure paths are exercisable without a real gqlgen install; `isolateTempDir` points `TMPDIR` at `t.TempDir()` to scope leak assertions. `merge_test.go` covers the §26.5.6 collision rules (consumer-wins on `UUID`, hand-bound `Product`, schema-glob promotion + dedup) plus the malformed-YAML error path. `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/wrapper 3.0s) and `make check-examples` (7 example modules, all green) pass cleanly. **16.1 surface delta unchanged from 2026-04-29:** Surface delta: (a) **runtime types** (`types/json.go`, `types/datetime.go`) gain stdlib-only `MarshalGQL(io.Writer)` / `UnmarshalGQL(any) error` on `JSONMap`, `DateTime`, `NullDateTime`. NullDateTime emits literal `null` when `Valid: false` and accepts `nil` to clear; DateTime accepts both RFC3339 and RFC3339Nano. (b) **Config** (`cmd/sqlgen/config/config.go`) gains `APIConfig` / `GraphQLAPIConfig` / `ScalarBinding` / `RESTAPIConfig` / `GRPCAPIConfig` plus per-table `TableAPIConfig` (tri-state `Enabled *bool`); `applyAPIDefaults` applies the §26.3 defaults (`max_depth=5`, `max_complexity=1000`, `field_casing=camel_case`, `gqlgen_bin="go run github.com/99designs/gqlgen"`, etc.). `validateAPIConfig` rejects unknown `field_casing` values and any scalar that omits or mis-spells `marshaling:`. (c) **Codegen** (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/context_api.go` — new) defines `APIContext` / `APITableContext` / `APIFieldContext` / `APIRelationshipContext` / `APIScalarUse` plus a built-in scalar registry (`types.JSONMap`/`types.DateTime`/`types.NullDateTime` → method, `uuid.UUID`/`decimal.Decimal`/`json.RawMessage` → external, `time.Time` → bundled). Per-table builder walks `[]TableContext`, emits PK-rule-aware GraphQL types (`ID!` for string PKs, `Int!` for numeric PKs, scalar names from the registry for non-spec types), and accumulates a globally-deduped scalar set. (d) **Templates** (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/templates/api/`) — `schema.graphqls.tmpl` per-table (type + Connection + Edge + ListResult + Filter + Sort + SortField + Create / Update inputs + Query / Mutation extensions, with `_inc`/`_dec` paired fields on every numeric Update field per §26.5.4); `shared.graphqls.tmpl` with the bare Query/Mutation roots, PageInfo, SortDirection, comparator family inputs, and `scalar X` declarations; `scalars.go.tmpl` emits external `MarshalX`/`UnmarshalX` for category-4 scalars only when the schema actually uses them. (e) **Orchestration** (`cmd/sqlgen/gen/orchestrate.go`) gains `generateAPI` step gated on `cfg.API.Enabled && cfg.API.GraphQL.Enabled`; writes per-table + shared `.graphqls` files (raw text) plus `scalars_gen.go` (formatted) under `schema_dir`/`resolver_dir`. (f) **Funcmap** gains `comparatorFor`, `screamingSnakeCase`, `hasAnyMutation` helpers. **Three test-author reconciliations documented inline at phase-16.md:** (1) default numeric binding is `Float` (`Decimal` requires `overrides.types`); (2) default timestamp binding is `Time` (gqlgen-bundled, `DateTime` requires `overrides.types` to `types.DateTime`); (3) string-bound PK columns surface as `ID!` per the GraphQL spec rule, not the registry-bound scalar — the §26.4 row reads "when the resolved Go type is uuid.UUID" so the default `string` binding lands on `ID!`. **Tests:** `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_schema_test.go` (4 tests covering type emission, casing, exclusion, scalar dedup) + `cmd/sqlgen/gen/api_scalars_test.go` (4 tests covering category-4 marshaler emission, codegen-time + config-time scalar-without-marshaling rejection, bad field_casing rejection) + `types/types_gqlgen_test.go` (5 tests covering JSONMap / DateTime / NullDateTime round-trip + error paths). `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green) and `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all E2E `-race` tests green) pass cleanly. **Next:** 16.4 (Field selection translator — the central walker per PRD §26.5.2, recursive descent + walker-completeness lint + connection unwrap helper).

**Phase 15 (Stream + LockMode) closed on 2026-04-29.** All 6 sub-items complete (15.1–15.6 landed across 2026-04-28 → 2026-04-29). 15.6 phase-closure sweep ran `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; wall 1:10; slowest cmd/sqlgen/cli 44.6s), `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all E2E `-race` tests green; wall 53s; mysql 12.9s + postgres 11.4s slowest), and `make test-integration` (28 modules, full non-short `-race` with 10m per-module timeout, all green; wall 2:56; cmd/sqlgen 136.8s + cmd/sqlgen/cli 83.3s + cmd/sqlgen/gen 12.1s + parser/introspect 11.4s slowest). The integration run confirms FIX-057 is holding through the Phase-15 changes — `parser/introspect.TestMySQLIntrospect` and `_TestMySQLIntrospectViews` both pass under the `enum`/`set` rewrite. **No new `/fix` entries filed during Phase 15** — `fixes.md` "Open" section remains empty. **Three targeted PRD edits** reconciling §9.4a / §9.6a wording against landed mechanism (no behavioral changes — clarifications only): (1) §9.4a Hook integration: rewrote the cache-hook sentence to describe the actual `cacheSkipper` short-circuit on `SkipCache=true` rather than the (non-existent) `OpStream`-specific bypass that the original wording implied. Verified by grepping `cache.go.tmpl` — no `OpStream` references; the existing interface at line 654-659 + check at line 671 carries the load. (2) §9.6a MySQL version requirement: replaced the parenthetical "Phase-15+ implementations: detect at client init" with a concrete description of the landed lazy-probe mechanism — `sync.Once`-gated `SELECT VERSION()` on first `LockForUpdateNoWait`/`SkipLocked` call, error-cached-across-callers behavior, postgres+sqlite client structs explicitly excluded, MariaDB compatibility note (`10.x-MariaDB` parses to major 10, trivially clears the `< 8` check). Pinned by `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/mysql/models/mysql_version_test.go::TestMySQLVersionCache_singleProbe` / `_errorCachedAcrossCalls` / `_parsesMajorVersion/mariadb_10.11.6`. (3) §9.6a Chained internal calls: PRD originally addressed only write-method chained Get sites; expanded to cover the dual invariant — write methods (Create/Update/Upsert/Restore/SoftDelete*) FORCE `LockMode=LockNone` on internal Get/GetMany; read methods (Get→GetMany, Connection→GetMany) PROPAGATE the caller's LockMode so the inner SELECT actually emits the lock clause. Both invariants pinned by `lock_mode_codegen_test.go::TestChainedGet_LockNoneForced` (write) and `_PaginationDoesNotForceLockNone` (read). **Test-only drift inventory** (closed at landing — listed for reference): 15.3 cache-bypass not E2E-exercised on postgres/mysql (pinned by 15.2 codegen test + cache/tests/skip_test.go); 15.3 tenant-scoped locking unobservable on tenancy example (sqlite-backed; pinned by 15.2 codegen test); 15.3 SoftDelete/Restore × tenant-in-PK composite-PK gap (only such table is order_items with no soft-delete column); 15.5 cache-bypass not E2E-exercised on postgres/mysql/sqlite (same root cause as 15.3); 15.5 mid-stream connection failure exercised via `stubFailRows` driver-independent contract; 15.5 memory-bound assertion comparative + postgres-only (mysql/sqlite drivers buffer the result set). **Phase-15 surface delta:** 5 new `LockMode` constants, 1 new `Dialect.LockClause()` method, 1 new `database.InTransaction()` helper, 1 new `hook.OpStream` op, 1 new generated `mysqlVersion` field per MySQL entity client, 1 new generated `Stream` method per non-view table, 4 new generated types per non-view table (`Stream{Table}{Plural}Input` + `Stream{Table}FieldOptions` + `scan{Table}Row` + augmented `CallOptions[FO]` carrying `LockMode`), 1 new `operations.stream` config field. Backwards-compatible at the consumer surface (zero-value LockMode = LockNone preserves prior behavior); breaking only for custom-dialect implementers (no such users in the example set). **Next:** post-Phase-15 fix-implement batch (none open) plus the deferred Phase 12.12 dev-tooling work.

**Phase 15 (Stream + LockMode) started 2026-04-28.** 15.1 + 15.2 + 15.3 + 15.4 + 15.5 complete (15.5 landed 2026-04-29). 15.5 (Stream E2E) added 4 new test files exercising PRD §9.4a end-to-end across postgres / mysql / sqlite / tenancy example modules: (a) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/postgres/tests/stream_test.go` — 8 tests using the postgres:16-alpine testcontainer: happy-path baseline (50 rows, no duplicates), filter / sort propagation captured via `recordingPgxQuerier` (WHERE + ORDER BY + DESC; `LIMIT` / `OFFSET` confirmed absent per PRD §9.4a), scalar-only column selection (`StreamFieldOptions{ID: true, Title: true}` produces `SELECT "id", "title"`; yielded entity has only those fields populated), soft-delete hook composition (default exclusion + `Filter.DeletedAt` override), early-break cleanup (5 partial streams; pgxpool's `AcquiredConns` returns to baseline), mid-stream cancellation (relaxed contract — pgx may buffer small result sets), driver-independent row-failure pin via `stubFailRows` (forces `rows.Err()` to return a sentinel after N successful Next() calls; Stream emits `(nil, errStubMidStreamFailure)` and terminates), and memory-bounded iteration (2k-row backlog; `streamAlloc <= 2 * getManyAlloc` via `runtime.MemStats.TotalAlloc` comparison; skipped under `-short`). (b) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/mysql/tests/stream_test.go` — 6 tests mirroring postgres against mysql:8.0 testcontainer; identifier-quote uses MySQL backticks (`\`title\``); per-driver buffering caveat documented in file header (stdlib mysql buffers full result set client-side; memory-bound assertion intentionally not mirrored). (c) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/sqlite/tests/stream_test.go` — 6 tests mirroring postgres adapted for modernc.org/sqlite (in-process driver — same buffering caveat; memory-bound assertion not mirrored). (d) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/tests/stream_test.go` — 3 tests pinning §29 + §9.4a composition: tenant filter narrows the streamed rows (5 A + 3 B seeded; tenant-A-bound client streams only A's 5; every yielded `WorkspaceID == tenantA`); `SkipTenancy: true` on Stream returns all 8 rows across both tenants (counts pinned at {A:5, B:3}); missing-resolver fail-closed (`tenancy.ErrMissing` surfaces, 0 rows yielded, counter pinned at 0 Query ops — fail-closed must short-circuit before SQL). Each example module defines its own `recording*Querier` (postgres/pgx, mysql/stdlib, sqlite/stdlib) — duplication is structural since example modules have separate go.mods (GOWORK=off build); same convention as 15.3's lock_mode_test.go files. **Three scope drifts documented inline at file headers:** (1) **Cache-bypass:** postgres / mysql / sqlite have no cache wired (`sqlgen.yml` does not enable it); SkipCache force is pinned by 15.4 codegen test (`stream_codegen_test.go::TestStream_MethodBodyEmission`) + `cache/tests/skip_test.go` runtime test. The Stream method's `options.SkipCache = true` lands in the `&hook.QueryContext{... CallOptions: options}` snapshot the cache hook reads, so PRD §27.7 holds end-to-end. Wiring a cache module locally for one E2E test is out of scope per the Phase-14 test-only rule. (2) **Mid-stream connection failure — driver-independent contract:** ctx cancellation can't reliably surface mid-stream because pgx buffers small result sets, stdlib mysql buffers the entire result set client-side, and modernc.org/sqlite is in-process (cancellation may lose the race against buffer drain). Strict pin uses `stubFailRows` in postgres — a `database.Rows` wrapper that forces `Err()` to return a sentinel after N successful Next() calls; Stream's post-loop `if err := rows.Err(); err != nil { yield(nil, err) }` is the load-bearing line. Driver-specific cancellation tests use the relaxed "if observed, must be ctx error" contract to absorb buffering variability. (3) **Memory-bound — comparative, not absolute:** Stream's TotalAlloc is compared to GetMany's TotalAlloc on the same backlog with a 2× ceiling; lenient because pgx still allocates per-row scan buffers. A regression that materialized inside Stream would inflate this far beyond 2×. Skipped under `-short` (timing-sensitive). MySQL/SQLite drivers buffer the result set, which would dominate the measurement and produce a noisy signal — postgres-only. **One landed-behavior reconciliation surfaced during test bring-up (not a bug):** the articles client's default `queryLimit = 1000` truncates GetMany results when no explicit `Limit` is passed; the memory-bound test passes `Limit: &n` (n = 2000) to bypass the cap. Documented inline at the test. **Tenancy guard ordering** pinned by `TestStream_MissingResolverFailsClosed`: in-method tenant resolution runs BEFORE BuildSelect / conn.Query (counter records 0 Query ops attributable to the failed Stream). `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/cli at 43.1s) and `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all `-race` E2E tests green; mysql 12.0s, postgres 5.4s — the 4 new stream tests added ~0.7s dominated by the 2k-row seed; postgres_stdlib 2.9s, tenancy 2.0s, sqlite 2.1s, cache 1.8s, events 1.5s) pass cleanly. No bugs uncovered; no `/fix` filed. **Next:** 15.6 (phase closure — full integration sweep, PRD §9.4a / §9.6a reconciliation against landed codegen, STATUS.md Current Focus update).

**Phase 15 (Stream + LockMode) started 2026-04-28.** 15.1 + 15.2 + 15.3 complete (15.3 landed 2026-04-29). 15.3 (LockMode E2E) added 4 new test files exercising PRD §9.6a end-to-end across postgres / mysql / sqlite / tenancy example modules: (a) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/postgres/tests/lock_mode_test.go` — 5 tests using the postgres:16-alpine testcontainer: read-modify-write inside `WithTx` with a concurrent contender that blocks until outer commit and then observes the post-commit value; `LockForUpdateNoWait` against a held row surfaces SQLSTATE 55P03 via `errors.As(*pgconn.PgError)`; 20-article `LockForUpdateSkipLocked` job queue split 10/10 between two workers (sort + duplicate scan pins zero overlap); table-driven outside-tx guard across 4 LockMode constants pinned at 0 db ops via `countingPgxQuerier`; outer-tx FOR UPDATE survives inner-savepoint rollback (concurrent NOWAIT contender still hits 55P03 inside the outer tx body). (b) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/mysql/tests/lock_mode_test.go` — 5 tests mirroring postgres against the mysql:8.0 testcontainer; ER_LOCK_NOWAIT (3572) pinned via `errors.As(*mysql.MySQLError)`; 15s timeouts on contender selects to absorb MySQL's slightly-longer lock wait under testcontainer load. (c) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/sqlite/tests/lock_mode_test.go` — single table-driven test `TestLockMode_SQLiteRejectsAllNonNoneModes` covering 4 modes × 4 method shapes (Get/outside_tx, Get/inside_tx, GetMany/outside_tx, Connection/outside_tx) = 16 sub-cases; each pins the SQLite-specific error string AND zero SELECT-class ops via `countingSqliteQuerier`. (d) `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/tests/lock_mode_test.go` — 2 tests pinning the **guard ordering invariant**: SQLite LockMode rejection fires before the tenant resolver is consulted (panicking-resolver test records 0 calls; missing-resolver test confirms `tenancy.ErrMissing` does NOT escape — SQLite rejection wins to the surface). Each test file defines its own `counting*Querier` (postgres/pgx, mysql/stdlib, sqlite/stdlib, tenancy/stdlib) — duplication is structural since example modules have separate go.mods (GOWORK=off build); shared helpers live in cache/ and tenancy/ main_test.go. **Two scope drifts documented inline at file headers:** (1) Cache-bypass: postgres / mysql examples have no cache wired in `sqlgen.yml`; SkipCache force is pinned by 15.2's codegen test (`lock_mode_codegen_test.go`) + cache/tests/skip_test.go runtime test. Wiring a cache locally would require codegen regeneration outside test-only scope. (2) Tenancy tenant-scoped locking: tenancy module is sqlite-backed (per main_test.go header), so SQLite rejects all LockMode values before the tenancy hook runs; cross-dialect tenant + LockMode composition is pinned at codegen layer in 15.2. **Three NoWait error reconciliations (PRD-aligned, no driver-mapping changes):** (a) postgres 55P03 surfaces unchanged through `database/pgx/errors.go::MapError` (which only translates 23xxx constraint codes + 40P01 deadlock + 08xxx connection); (b) mysql 3572 surfaces unchanged through `database/stdlib/errors_postgres.go::mapMySQLError` (only translates 1062/1451/1452/1048/3819/1213); (c) tests pin via numeric error code, not message format, so driver locale changes don't break the contract. **Connection input shape:** sqlite test required `models.ConnectionInput[models.CategoryFilter]` — generated input type is a generic alias parameterised by the filter, not a per-table `Categories­ConnectionInput` (pinned at sqlite/models/connection_gen.go:13). `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; cmd/sqlgen/cli at 36.5s) and `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all E2E `-race` tests green; mysql 9.0s, postgres 3.4s, postgres_stdlib 3.1s, tenancy 2.0s, cache 1.9s, sqlite 1.8s, events 1.6s — the four new lock_mode_test.go files added ~0.4s aggregate runtime) pass cleanly. No bugs uncovered; no `/fix` filed. **Next:** 15.4 (Stream codegen — types, method body, `OpStream` hook op, `operations.stream` config field).

**Phase 14 (E2E Coverage Expansion) closed on 2026-04-27.** All 13 sub-items complete. 14.13 phase-closure sweep ran `make check` (8 modules, 0 lint, all `-short -race` unit tests green; slowest cmd/sqlgen/cli at 37.1s), `make check-examples` (7 example modules, 0 lint, all E2E `-race` tests green; mysql 11.5s, others 1.6–3.1s), and `make test-integration` (full non-short `-race` across runtime + parser + cmd). The integration sweep surfaced **one pre-existing failure** — `parser/introspect.TestMySQLIntrospect` line 365 reports `got "enum", want "users_status_enum"`. Not a Phase-14 regression: the failing test was added in cf68926 (Phase 4 introspect feature, 2026-04-09) and only runs under `make test-integration` (skipped by `make check`'s `-short` gate); MySQL introspector emits the bare `"enum"` keyword and synthesises enum names without a table prefix, drifting from `parser/mysql/mysql.go::extractInlineEnumOrSet` which uses table-scoped `<table>_<col>_enum`. Filed as **FIX-057** (open) and tracked for a post-Phase-14 fix-implement run; out of the test-only Phase-14 scope. Phase-14 invariants honoured: test-only scope rule held for 14.1–14.10 and 14.12 (FIX-053/054/055/056 edits live under their own fix entries, not under the surfacing sub-item); 14.9 added one schema column under the explicit "if schema additions are required" task bullet.
