**Phase 13 (Tenancy) is in progress.** 13.1–13.10 complete (13.1–13.7 on 2026-04-22; 13.8–13.10 on 2026-04-23). 13.10 reconciled implementation back into the PRD after a full audit of 13.1–13.9 findings against §29. The only load-bearing drift was §29.7 — the original "constructor omits the tenant field" wording diverged from the landed codegen, which uses the Option-2 verify-match rule (tenant stays on `XXXPK`; `Get`/`Update`/`HardDelete`/`Increment`/`Exists` reject `pk.Tenant != resolved` with `tenancy.ErrMismatch` before the DB round-trip). Rewrote §29.7 to match and added a rationale paragraph explaining why verify-match won over omit-from-signature (round-trip symmetry, uniform cache-key shape, SkipTenancy ergonomics). Strengthened the `Upsert` bullet in §29.4.2 to call out the conflict-clause UPDATE-set tenant exclusion (the FIX-050 invariant made explicit, since the original wording left the `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE SET` / `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` projection ambiguous). Expanded `docs/design/TENANCY.md` §5.3 with an implementation-notes block capturing the verify-match decision trail and the zero-value-is-mismatch property. Verified `tenancy/tenancy.go` godoc aligns verbatim with §29.3.1 (error strings byte-identical; `T comparable` rationale paragraph matches) — no code edits. Verified cross-reference sites §4.8, §9.6, §17, §27.5, §28.3 already name tenancy and link to §29 — added during 13.1–13.9 incrementally; 13.10 confirmed no orphan `tenancy`/`Tenancy` references in PRD (every mention either lives in §29 or points at §29). FIX-049 (o2o chained-alias bug surfaced during 13.9) was already resolved in its own commit before 13.10 started; no PRD design note needed — the fix preserves the §29.10 belt-and-suspenders invariant rather than amending it. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues (docs-only changes). 13.11 is a stretch lint-rule item gated on Phase 12.12 infrastructure (currently deferred); Phase 13 is functionally closed.

**Phase 14 (E2E Coverage Expansion) started 2026-04-23.** 14.1 (`Increment` e2e) complete: 26 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite,tenancy}/tests/increment_test.go`. Postgres/mysql/sqlite cover positive, negative, and zero deltas across integer and numeric/decimal/real columns on `products` and `order_items`, plus `ErrNotFound` under the default `strict_updates: true` and idempotent nil-return under a new `strict_updates: false` config on `orders`. Tenancy covers: simple-PK tenant filter auto-applied (cross-tenant Increment surfaces `ErrNotFound`, target row untouched); SkipTenancy admin path succeeds across tenants; composite-PK verify-match on `order_items` (resolver-vs-PK mismatch, zero-UUID PK, SkipTenancy escape); §29.3.1 fail-closed on missing tenant. MySQL zero-delta case uses `orders.total` (strict_updates=false) instead of `products.price` to sidestep the go-sql-driver/mysql rows-CHANGED vs. rows-MATCHED quirk — documented inline in the test comment rather than changing the mysql connection string (scope). Regenerated postgres/mysql/sqlite models + expected via `make update-golden-e2e`; diff localized to the `orders` client. `make check` and `make lint-examples` pass with 0 issues.

**14.2 (`Exists` / `ExistsWhere` e2e) complete 2026-04-23.** 34 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite,tenancy}/tests/exists_test.go` (9 + 9 + 9 + 7). Postgres/mysql/sqlite cover: present/absent PK → `bool`; `ExistsWhere` with `In`, `Gte`, `Like`, and `Null`/`NotNull` operators; nil-filter legal-probe behavior; default soft-delete exclusion on `Exists`; `Filter.DeletedAt` override on `ExistsWhere`. Tenancy covers: cross-tenant `Exists`/`ExistsWhere` return false (simple-PK tenant filter auto-applied); `SkipTenancy` admin escape for both variants; composite-PK verify-match on `Exists` (resolver-vs-PK mismatch, zero-UUID PK, SkipTenancy); §29.3.1 fail-closed on both `Exists` and `ExistsWhere`. **Phase-doc reconciliation:** two bullets in `docs/tracker/phase-14.md` §14.2 did not match landed behavior — (1) "ErrEmptyFilter from ExistsWhere" is not implemented (PRD §22 scopes that sentinel to mutation *Where ops only; ExistsWhere is a read-only scalar probe and `ExistsWhere(ctx, nil)` is legal); (2) there is no `SkipSoftDelete` CallOption — soft-delete bypass uses `Filter.DeletedAt` comparator per §9.8.10 godoc. Both drifts documented as scope notes atop each new test file; no code change needed (tests validate the actual, PRD-consistent behavior). `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues.

**14.3 (`*Where` mutation edge cases) complete 2026-04-23.** 41 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite,cache,events,tenancy}/tests/where_mutations_test.go` (9 + 9 + 9 + 5 + 5 + 4). Postgres/mysql/sqlite each cover: empty-filter (`&Filter{}`) → `ErrEmptyFilter` for all four `*Where` ops; zero-match → empty slice / nil error for each op; `SoftDeleteWhere` against already-soft-deleted rows is a no-op (the op auto-scopes to `deleted_at IS NULL`). Cache covers: `UpdateWhere` / `SoftDeleteWhere` / `RestoreWhere` / `HardDeleteWhere` each fire exactly one `InvalidatePattern` regardless of affected-row count (3 / 2 rows); zero-match `UpdateWhere` still fires the pattern (the op cannot know affected count without round-tripping). The `HardDeleteWhere` cache test uses `articles` (no dependent view) to isolate table-pattern from view-cascade — the cache fixture's `product_summary` view has `invalidate_on: [products]` so mutations on `products` legitimately fire 2 patterns; using `articles` keeps the "exactly 1" assertion meaningful. Events covers: N=5 / N=3 affected rows fan out to N events for each `*Where` op; `Event.Input` carries the operation-specific input by reference per §28.6 — `*UpdateInput` for `UpdateWhere` (the SET payload — the filter is the row selector, not the input), `*Filter` for the three other ops (no separate input). Zero-match → zero events. Tenancy covers: `UpdateWhere` with caller-set `WorkspaceID` mismatching the resolver returns `tenancy.ErrMismatch` with zero DB ops (verified via `countingQuerier`); resolver tenant AND'd into the final `WHERE` for `UpdateWhere` / `SoftDeleteWhere` / `HardDeleteWhere` — verified by row-count assertion (seed cross-tenant rows with the same predicate; assert tenant B's row untouched after tenant A's `*Where` op). **`Event.Input` shape note** documented inline: `UpdateWhere` carries `*UpdateInput` (not the filter) — matches §28.6 "the same value the caller passed", which for `UpdateWhere` is the SET payload while the filter is the predicate. Phase-14 scope rule honored — test-only changes; no generator/template/runtime/schema changes. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues; all 41 new tests pass under `-race`.

**14.4 (`Connection` cursor pagination e2e) complete 2026-04-24.** 27 new tests + 15 subtests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite,tenancy}/tests/connection_test.go` (6 + 6 + 6 + 3 top-level tests; postgres/mysql/sqlite each add 5 subtests on the tampered-cursor table-driven case). Postgres/mysql/sqlite each cover: full-table forward traversal of a seeded 50-row set in pages of 10 (every row visited exactly once, no duplicates); full-table backward traversal via `Last`+`Before` (every row visited exactly once); opaque cursor round-trip byte-equality (decode base64 → JSON-unmarshal → re-marshal → re-encode base64 == original cursor, verifying deterministic encoding per PRD §14.2); tampered cursor → `ErrInvalidCursor` under 5 tamper modes (non-base64 After, non-JSON After, non-base64 Before, non-JSON Before, `First`+`Last` both set); `PageInfo.HasNextPage`/`HasPreviousPage`/`StartCursor`/`EndCursor` correctness at first / middle / last / empty-result pages; 3-column composite-PK cursor on `product_tag_labels` (cursor_keys explicit in sqlgen.yml as `[product_id, tag_name, label]`) exercising the row-value keyset comparator `(a,b,c) > (v1,v2,v3)` under ties on `product_id` (two products each own multiple labels — tag_name/label must break ties for determinism). Tenancy covers: interleaved 15 A + 15 B rows on `products` (simple PK, cursor_keys default `["id"]`), tenant A's forward Connection walks in pages of 4 and every page contains only tenant A rows (asserted per-edge via `WorkspaceID == tenantA`, not just count); backward traversal mirror; `SkipTenancy:true` admin path surfaces both tenants' rows with `TotalCount = 2*perTenant`. **Phase-doc reconciliation (one drift, not a bug):** §14.4 originally named `order_items` as the 3-column-composite cursor target, but in the postgres example schema `order_items` has a single-UUID PK — the 3-column-composite table that actually exists is `product_tag_labels`. Tests use `product_tag_labels` and cite the rationale at the top of each file; phase-14.md task bullet amended to name the actual table. Phase-14 scope rule (test-only) honored: no generator / template / runtime / schema / config changes. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; full E2E suite runs clean under `-race`.

**14.5 (filter operator breadth) complete 2026-04-24.** 25 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite}/tests/filter_test.go` (postgres 10 new, extending the existing file; mysql 8 new in a brand-new file; sqlite 7 new in a brand-new file). Each dialect module adds a local `capturingQuerier` helper (inline per-module since the three modules are independent Go modules) that records every SQL string passed to `Query`/`QueryRow`/`Exec`; the helper backs the `ByteIdenticalSQL` test which double-invokes GetMany with the same filter-value set and asserts the last captured SELECT is byte-identical (regression guard for PRD §11.1 ToConditions determinism and placeholder-numbering stability). Operator coverage per PRD §11.2: `Like`/`NLike` with literal `%` and `_` patterns across dialects; `Null`/`NotNull` on nullable `articles.body`; `Between`/`NBetween` on numeric (`products.price`) and timestamp (`articles.created_at`) columns; `Custom` with ≥2 positional placeholders via `sql.Raw`; 3-condition `And` composition on `ProductFilter` plus nested `Or`-inside-`And`. Postgres-only: `ILIKE` via the `Custom` escape hatch (ILike is not a first-class String operator per §11.2); `Slice[string]` `ContainsAny`/`ContainsAll`/`IsEmpty` on `products.tags` (TEXT[]); JSONB `@>` containment via Custom (single + compound) — the generated `ProductFilter.Metadata` is typed `*NullableString`, so JSONB operators thread through `sql.Raw` rather than a dedicated JSONB-comparator field. MySQL: `JSON_CONTAINS(attributes, $)` via Custom on the String-typed `Attributes` filter field.

**Scope / design notes documented inline:** (1) `ILike` is not in the `comparator.String` operator set (PRD §11.2); tests use `Custom` + `sql.Raw("col ILIKE $", …)` per §11.4 — no code change needed. (2) The codegen maps `jsonb` (PG) and `json` (MySQL) columns to `NullableString`/`String` in filter structs rather than auto-selecting `JSONB`/`JSON` comparators. The test exercises the landed behavior; revisiting auto-selection is out of Phase-14 scope. (3) ~~**Or-of-two-conditions requires one sub-filter, not two.**~~ **Superseded by FIX-195 (2026-09-10)** — the rule is now the opposite. Pre-fix, the generated `ToConditions` wrapped each `f.Or[i]` in its own `sql.Or(subConds...)`, so two sub-filters with one condition each became two OR-of-one wrappers AND'd together rather than `(A OR B)`, and the workaround was to put both branches on ONE sub-filter. Under amended PRD §11.1 each member contributes `sql.And` of its own fields and the members are OR'd as one group, so `(A OR B)` is one branch per member and `{Price: {Lt: &twenty, Gt: &hundred}}` is the unsatisfiable conjunction. The dialect tests assert both halves. Phase-14 scope rule (test-only) honored: no generator / template / runtime / schema / config changes. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules.

**14.6 (tenancy × relationships — O2O chain + M2M junction variance) complete 2026-04-24.** 7 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/tests/{relationship_chain_test.go,m2m_junction_test.go}` (4 chain + 3 junction). Chain file adds a local `capturingQuerier` (records SQL text; the shared `countingQuerier` records counts only) and a `countWorkspaceIDPredicates` helper that slices to WHERE-onwards before counting the `workspace_id = ` token — matches both alias-qualified chain predicates (`u.workspace_id = ?`) and unqualified single-table predicates without double-counting the projected column. Chain tests exercise §29.10: `TestO2OChain_SingleQueryForChainedLoad` (exactly one SELECT on users for a 3-chain-level fieldOptions selection — §13.2 flattens O2O chains into one multi-JOIN); `TestO2OChain_TenantWHEREOnEveryAlias` (captured SELECT has 4 workspace_id predicates — parent u + chained p, us, pr — verified per-alias); `TestO2OChain_SkipTenancyDropsFilterOnEveryAlias` (SkipTenancy:true → zero workspace_id predicates in captured SELECT); `TestO2OChain_CrossTenantRowsFilteredAtEveryChainLevel` (behavioural mirror — seed cross-tenant poison, verify all 3 chain levels return tenant A's rows only). Junction tests exercise §29.10 + §29.5: `TestM2M_NonTenantedJunctionSQLShape` (post_tags SELECT contains zero `workspace_id` tokens — the table has no such column; tags child SELECT carries the tenant predicate; parent posts SELECT carries the tenant predicate); `TestM2M_ParentCacheKeyIsTenantScoped` and `TestM2M_ChildCacheKeyIsTenantScoped` (BuildTenantTablePattern(posts|tags, A) + InvalidatePattern evicts only A's entries; B's entries on the shared backend still HIT — proves both parent posts and child tags cache keys carry the tenant segment). **Phase-doc reconciliation (1 drift — not a code bug):** §14.6 originally asked for a new three-table A→B→C chain (all tenanted). The landed generator emits O2O JOINs for every chain level auto-detected through FK back-references (FIX-049 context), producing alias sequence `u → p → us → pr → use → pro → user`; selecting `fo.Profile.Users.Profile` materialises 3 chained aliases (p, us, pr) on top of parent u → 4 tenant conditions in outer WHERE, structurally identical to a 3-distinct-table chain for the §29.10 invariant under test. Sticking with the existing schema preserved the Phase-14 test-only scope rule; the reconciliation is documented at the top of `relationship_chain_test.go`. The "optional tenanted-junction variant" bullet was deferred for the same reason — the tenancy example has one non-tenanted junction (`post_tags`), and a tenanted-junction variant would require a schema migration + golden regen for coverage already delivered by generator unit tests. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 7 new tests pass under `-race`.

**FIX-053 resolved 2026-04-24 (post-14.7).** `tenancy.required: false` previously did not skip the tenant filter on a zero-value resolver return, contradicting PRD §29.2's "missing tenant skips the filter" clause. Resolved by changing `resolveTenant`'s signature from `(T, error)` to `(T, bool, error)` — the new `apply` bool is false under `required:false + zero resolver` and every template call site now gates the filter-append / verify-match / auto-set on it. Secondary strengthening: under `required:true`, a resolver returning `(zero, nil)` is now upgraded to `tenancy.ErrMissing` at the generator level (previously the resolver had to return ErrMissing explicitly). Template changes span 9 files (`client.go.tmpl` plus every CRUD op template); all ~30 call sites use the new pattern. E2E coverage: `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/sqlgen.yml` adds per-table `tenancy.required: false` on `legacy_widgets`; new `required_false_test.go` adds 5 regression guards (zero-resolver filter-skip via capturingQuerier, non-zero-resolver filter-applied, resolver-error propagation, caller-supplied-OrgID Create, `required:true` negative control on products). The one breaking behavioural shift — previously `TestIsolation_NonNilUUIDOnly` asserted that a `uuid.Nil` resolver under `required:true` could still write rows and that other tenants couldn't see them; now such writes fail-closed with `tenancy.ErrMissing` — renamed to `TestIsolation_NilResolverFailsClosed` and updated to assert the new (stronger) contract. `make update-golden` + `make update-golden-e2e` regenerated all affected goldens; diff localized to the resolveTenant bodies + every `if !options.SkipTenancy` block gaining an inner `if apply` guard. `make check` + `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all tenancy tests pass under `-race`.

**14.7 (tenancy error & composite-PK mismatch sweep) complete 2026-04-24.** 33 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/tests/{errors_test.go,composite_pk_mismatch_test.go}` (20 + 13). `errors_test.go` sweeps the §29.3.1 fail-closed contract across 18 entry points on `articles` (simple PK, timestamp soft-delete — gives Restore in addition to SoftDelete/HardDelete) + `products` for Increment (articles has no numeric column): `Get / GetMany / Connection / Create / CreateMany / Update / UpdateMany / UpdateWhere / Upsert / HardDelete / HardDeleteWhere / SoftDelete / SoftDeleteWhere / Restore / RestoreWhere / Increment / Exists / ExistsWhere`. Each test swaps the default `staticResolver(tenantA)` for `missingResolver()` (returns `(uuid.Nil, tenancy.ErrMissing)`), asserts `errors.Is(err, tenancy.ErrMissing)` and zero DB ops via `countingQuerier`. Plus 2 bonus tests (`OrderItems.Get` / `OrderItems.Update`) confirming the sweep holds for the §29.7 verify-match path — when the resolver itself errors, that's what surfaces, not ErrMismatch. `composite_pk_mismatch_test.go` fills the §29.7 per-op gaps left by `composite_pk_test.go`: 2 Upsert mismatch tests (input.WorkspaceID vs resolver mismatch; zero-UUID on the INSERT input); 4 zero-value-on-PK sweep (Update / HardDelete / Increment / Exists — Get already covered); 5 SkipTenancy:true SQL-shape tests on every PK-scoped op reusing 14.6's `newCapturingClient` helper; 2 happy-path Upsert tests (SkipTenancy:true + matching tenant succeeds; SkipTenancy:true admin cross-tenant write succeeds). SQL-shape tests use a `pathOrderProduct(pathIdx)` helper so the two paths target distinct rows — same DB, no UNIQUE-constraint collision on the composite PK. **Three phase-doc reconciliations documented inline:** (1) **`required: false` bullet deferred — logged as FIX-053.** PRD §29.2 specifies `required: false` + zero-value resolver skips the tenant filter, but no template consumes the `TenancyContext.Required` flag (`context_tenancy.go:25, :142, :381` populate it; grep turns up zero template consumers). Every tenanted op unconditionally appends the tenant filter regardless of `Required`. Writing a test against the landed behavior would lock in the drift; writing against the PRD-specified behavior would fail on landed codegen. Phase-14 scope rule: test-only, no codegen changes — so the bullet is deferred until FIX-053 implements the gating. (2) **"Byte-identical SQL" reconciled to "same PK-predicate shape".** For composite-PK tables with tenant-in-PK, SkipTenancy:false has TWO `workspace_id = ?` predicates in WHERE (PK filter + GetMany auto-filter) and SkipTenancy:true has ONE (PK filter only). Both paths identify the same row deterministically; the DB optimizer collapses the duplicate. The redundant predicate is a correctness property — it keeps the tenant-in-WHERE invariant uniform across simple-PK and composite-PK tables. Tests assert the `(N=2, N=1)` distinction on every PK-scoped op so a refactor that drops either predicate surfaces as a visible diff. (3) **SoftDelete / Restore on composite-PK-with-tenant-in-PK deferred.** The only such table is `order_items`, which has no soft-delete column; adding one requires schema migration + golden regen, outside Phase-14 test-only scope. Simple-PK soft-delete × tenancy coverage already lives in `soft_delete_test.go` / `where_mutations_test.go`; the §29.7 verify-match rule is specific to the tenant-in-PK case. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 33 new tests pass under `-race`.

**14.12 (omittable — null-vs-unset, zero-change `Update` short-circuit) complete 2026-04-27.** 12 new top-level tests + 21 JSON sub-tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{postgres,mysql,sqlite}/tests/omittable_test.go` (4 top-level + 7 sub-tests per dialect). `TestUpdate_ZeroFields_NoUpdateSQL_<Dialect>` wraps `database.Querier` with the existing 14.5 `capturingQuerier`, calls `Update(id, &UpdateProductInput{})` with every field unset, and asserts no captured SQL string starts with `UPDATE ` — the `c.Get` short-circuit in §9.5 fires one SELECT and that's the only DB round-trip in the captured stream, exactly matching "no UPDATE statement is executed. The method fetches and returns the current entity unchanged." `TestUpdate_NullableColumn_OmitVsSetNil_<Dialect>` runs a 4-stage transition on `articles.body` (TEXT, nullable, no DEFAULT): seed `Set(&"initial")` → `Body=Omit()` (capture excludes `body` from SET; row preserves) → `Body=Set[*string](nil)` (capture includes `body`; row → NULL) → `Body=Set(&"replaced")` (capture includes; row → "replaced") → `Body=Omit()` again (capture excludes; row preserves "replaced"). Pins both directions of the null-vs-unset distinction at the SQL-text level AND the read-back level; postgres asserts `"body"`, mysql asserts `` `body` ``, sqlite asserts `"body"` per dialect identifier-quote. `TestCreate_OmitDefaultColumn_DBDefaultHonored_<Dialect>` asserts the captured INSERT column list excludes the omitted DEFAULT-bearing column AND the read-back row carries the DB default (postgres: `is_active`/`quantity`; mysql/sqlite: `in_stock`); negative control with `Set(false)` proves the omit-vs-explicit branch is real (column appears in INSERT, value is `false`). `TestOmittable_JSONRoundTrip_<Dialect>` — 7 sub-tests pinning the §10.4 contract: empty struct → `{}` (omitzero respected); Set fields present + unset fields absent in Marshal output; `Set[*string](nil)` → `"body":null`; absent JSON → IsSet()==false; present JSON → IsSet()==true with correct value; `null` for pointer T → `Set[*T](nil)` (IsSet()==true, MustGet returns nil pointer); full roundtrip preserves both Title (non-pointer Set) and Body (pointer Set) state, and unset fields stay unset. **Phase-14 scope rule honored** — no generator / template / runtime / schema / config changes; tests reuse the per-module `capturingQuerier` helpers from 14.5 + add local `lastUpdateSQL` / `lastInsertSQL` helpers per file (per-module duplication intentional, same pattern as 14.6 — the example modules are independent Go modules). No drift between PRD §9.5 / §10 and landed codegen — every assertion matches the generated method output verbatim. `make check` (lint + unit tests across runtime/parser/cmd) and `make check-examples` (lint + E2E tests across all 7 example modules: postgres / postgres_stdlib / mysql / sqlite / cache / events / tenancy) pass with 0 lint issues; all 12 new top-level tests and 21 JSON sub-tests pass under `-race`. Next: 14.13 (sync coverage expansion into tracker — phase-closure coordination sub-item).

**14.11 (events — tenant metadata, batch events, `Input` field) complete 2026-04-24.** 19 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/events/tests/{input_field_test.go,batch_test.go}` (14) and `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/tenancy/tests/event_metadata_test.go` (5). Surfaced **FIX-056** during initial test write-up — landed `*Many` codegen attached the full caller slice to every fanned-out event instead of the per-entity i-th element, contradicting PRD §28.6. Resolved before sub-item closure: `event_hooks.go.tmpl` rewritten with a per-op fanout switch (`OpCreateMany` / `OpUpdateMany` index `inputs[i]` / `items[i]`; `OpHardDeleteMany` / `OpSoftDeleteMany` / `OpRestoreMany` route to nil; default falls back to `mc.Input` for single-op + `*Where`); `context_event.go` grew `HasCreateMany` / `HasUpdateMany` flags so non-batch tables don't reference per-table types they never emit; `hook/hook.go` godoc on `MutationContext.Input` now documents the batch-vs-event two-surface contract; PRD §28.4 / §28.6 grew the per-entity-input paragraph + op→shape table. Tests now assert the corrected per-entity behavior. Events file: `input_field_test.go` fills the §28.6 type-assertion gaps left by existing `event_test.go` + `where_mutations_test.go` — `Upsert` (pointer equality across INSERT and DO-UPDATE), single-PK `SoftDelete` / `HardDelete` (landed `Event.Input == nil`), `Increment` (by-value `IncrementInput[C]`), `CreateMany` / `UpdateMany` (per-entity pointer/value equality against `inputs[i]` / `items[i]`), `SoftDeleteMany` / `HardDeleteMany` (`Event.Input == nil`), plus an 8-op `AllVariantsSweep` whose Create/Update arms accept both single-op and batch shapes uniformly (FIX-056 regression guard). `batch_test.go` pins two invariants existing tests left fuzzy: (1) event ordering follows caller's input slice (`UpdateMany` / `SoftDeleteMany` order matches reversed caller slice → proves hook iterates `AffectedPKs`, not a PK-sorted slice); (2) per-event distinctness (no dup PKs, every `Event.ID` is a unique UUID, every event carries correct Action/Table/Schema/Timestamp). Tenancy file: `event_metadata_test.go` covers angles the existing `event_test.go` doesn't — `NonTenantedTables_MultipleShapes` (audit_logs AND post_tags, distinct schema shapes); `SkipTenancy_AcrossActions` (key-absence holds for Update + HardDelete, not just Create); `BatchTenanted_UniformTenant` (CreateMany → N events byte-identical `Metadata["tenant"]` — the §29.6 "closed-over-once" property); `TenantedActions_CarryTenant` (tenant metadata on Update + HardDelete); and the acceptance-criteria regression guard `NoEmptyTenantStringRegression` pinning that `Metadata["tenant"] == ""` never leaks across tenanted / SkipTenancy / non-tenanted paths. **Two landed-behavior reconciliations documented inline** (`input_field_test.go` file header): (1) single-PK `SoftDelete` / `HardDelete` / `Restore` carry `Event.Input == nil` — the MutationContext literal leaves `Input` unset (only `PK` is set); the PK travels via the dedicated `Event.PK` field, and `*Many` delete-class ops route to nil through the same FIX-056 fanout switch. (2) `Increment` carries `IncrementInput[C]` by value (caller never took a pointer; value-assertion preserves Column + Amount equality). FIX-056 pulled this sub-item past the strict test-only scope rule (template + context_event.go + hook godoc + PRD edits + regenerated `event_hooks_gen.go` for events / tenancy / cache; postgres / postgres_stdlib / mysql / sqlite stayed byte-identical). `make check` + `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 19 new tests pass under `-race`. Next: 14.12 (omittable null-vs-unset + zero-change Update short-circuit).

**14.10 (cache fingerprint kill-switch + restore-after-soft-delete + view invalidation + tenancy hydration) complete 2026-04-24.** 10 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{cache,tenancy}/tests/` (cache 7 + tenancy 3). `cache/fingerprint_test.go` (2): seeds an old-fingerprint payload via `cache.BuildKey(..., "old00000", pk)` and asserts a normal client `Get` produces a different (live-fingerprint) key — the old payload remains in the backend (orphaned, awaiting LRU/TTL/pattern eviction) but is unreachable from the client; `InvalidateTable` (which goes through `BuildTablePattern`, fingerprint-and-pk-agnostic per §27.5) clears BOTH generations in one sweep, verified by post-invalidation `backend.Get` returning nil for the orphan AND the next live Get registering as a Miss. `cache/restore_test.go` (2): Cache(live) → `SoftDeleteWhere` (or single-PK `SoftDelete`) → Get returns `ErrNotFound` and writes nothing to the backend (§27.6 invariant: only full entities — absence is not cached) → `RestoreWhere` (or `Restore`) → Get round-trips DB and serves fresh row → follow-up Get hits cache. `cache/view_invalidation_test.go` (3): baseline `Create` on `products` fires one `invalidate_pattern` for `product_summary` with the §27.5-mandated fingerprint-and-pk-agnostic shape; t.Run matrix over `Create`/`Update`/`Upsert`/`HardDelete`/`HardDeleteWhere` confirms every mutation op dispatches the view-invalidation arm; negative guard — mutations on `articles` (NOT in `product_summary.invalidate_on`) do NOT fire the view pattern. `tenancy/cache_hydration_test.go` (3): tenant-A partial Get → background hydration writes one cache entry whose key contains `tenant:tenantA` and NOT `tenant:tenantB`; tenant-B Get on the same PK with the SHARED backend misses (different :tenant: key) and returns `ErrNotFound` from the DB-level filter — proves no cross-tenant cache leak; hydration on a soft-deleted row is a no-op (the `next(ctx, q)` returns `ErrNotFound` under the auto-applied `deleted_at IS NULL` filter, the `hydrateArticle` goroutine is never launched, `Set` count stays at 0); soft-delete → Restore cycle re-hydrates the live entity under the tenant-A key and a follow-up full Get hits cache. **Three phase-doc reconciliations documented inline:** (1) **"caches absence" wording reconciled.** PRD §27.6 is unambiguous — the cache only stores full entities. The phase-14 task wording was loose; what's actually under test is "no stale 'live' entity survives the soft-delete and `RestoreWhere` primes a clean lookup path", with each test cite §27.6 to forestall future test-author confusion. (2) **View read-through is NOT implemented in landed codegen.** Views go straight to the DB; only the *invalidation* arm of view caching is wired (the cache hook consults `patternsForSourceTable(table)` after a mutation on a source table). The §27.11 invariant we can verify end-to-end is the invalidation dispatch — hot-cache hits on views themselves would require a `readThrough{View}` codegen path, outside Phase-14 test-only scope. The view tests assert what's actually wired. (3) **Per-tenant view invalidation deferred.** The §29.5 acceptance bullet ("tenanted view + tenanted source: mutation by tenant A invalidates only tenant A's view cache entries") cannot be exercised in either example: cache example has views but no tenancy; tenancy example has tenancy but no views. Adding either combination is a schema migration + golden regen — outside Phase-14 test-only scope. The §29.5 `BuildTenantTablePattern` per-tenant grammar is already covered in `tenancy/tests/cache_test.go::TestCache_BuildTenantTablePattern{Format,InvalidatesOnlyOneTenant}` (tables, not views — helper is identical) and `cache/key_test.go`. Deferral documented inline at the top of `cache/tests/view_invalidation_test.go`. New `hydrationSpyBackend` helper added locally to the tenancy test file (the shared `spyMetrics` does not track hydration callbacks; the spy backend records `Set` calls so the test can poll for hydration completion deterministically). `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 10 new tests pass under `-race`. Next: 14.11 (events — tenant metadata, batch events, `Input` field).

**14.9 (nullable & array type-override round-trip) complete 2026-04-24.** 4 new tests appended to `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/postgres/tests/type_overrides_test.go`. (1) `TestNullableUUIDOverride_NullThenValueRoundTrip` — three-stage transition: Create-unset → `{Valid:false, UUID:uuid.Nil}` on RETURNING + Get readback; Update with `omittable.Set(uuid.NullUUID{UUID:v, Valid:true})` → asserts both return + readback; Update back to `omittable.Set(uuid.NullUUID{})` → asserts cleared on both paths (the nullable type's `driver.Valuer` returns `nil` for `!Valid`, writing SQL NULL). (2) `TestNullableDecimalOverride_NullThenValueRoundTrip` — mirror for `decimal.NullDecimal` with the additional `IsZero()` invariant on the embedded `Decimal` when `Valid:false` (catches stale-value bugs). (3) `TestKSUIDArrayOverride_RoundTrip` — exercises a new `tag_ids TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'` column on `warehouses` that maps to `[]ksuid.KSUID` via the existing per-table `text → ksuid.KSUID` override; covers Create with 3-element slice, empty-default Create (`len == 0`), Update-replace, and a per-element zero-KSUID guard against scan-loop element-drop. (4) `TestOverriddenTypes_EmptyGetManyNoPanic` — GetMany filtered on `external_id Eq <fresh KSUID>` (UNIQUE column → guaranteed zero matches) returns empty slice + nil error without panicking on the override-typed scan path. **Schema addition (in scope per the §14.9 task bullet):** `tag_ids TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'` + `COMMENT ON COLUMN warehouses.tag_ids` added to `postgres/schema.sql`; `make update-golden-e2e` regenerated `models/` + `expected/` (diff localized to the warehouses client adding `TagIDS` field, scan branch, columns slice, Create/Update set-clauses, Filter `*comparator.Slice[ksuid.KSUID]`, FieldOptions, and Get/Update/Upsert input plumbing). **Phase-doc reconciliation (one minor — landed behavior matches PRD):** the §14.9 acceptance criterion "not `[][]byte` or `pgtype.Array`" was a "what we want to avoid" statement; pgx5 scans `TEXT[]` into `[]ksuid.KSUID` via element-wise `sql.Scanner` invocation — no custom array scanner needed. Documented inline at the top of `TestKSUIDArrayOverride_RoundTrip`. **No `*T` integration types:** the existing config only declares `nullable: uuid.NullUUID` / `nullable: decimal.NullDecimal`; `tag_ids` is `NOT NULL` so no nullable variant is required. Verified via grep on `models_gen.go` that no `*ksuid.KSUID` / `*decimal.Decimal` / `*uuid.UUID` field exists for any warehouse column (only `*WarehouseFilter` / `*comparator.X` / `*Warehouse` pointers, which are aggregate / pointer-to-result types, not integration-type pointers). Phase-14 scope rule (test-only) honoured for the test portion; the schema + golden regen falls under the explicit "If schema additions are required..." task bullet. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 4 new tests pass under `-race`. Next: 14.10 (cache fingerprint kill-switch + view/tenancy invalidation).

**14.8 (transactions — deferred events, savepoints, batch partial failure) complete 2026-04-24.** 17 new tests across `cmd/sqlgen/testdata/examples/{events,postgres,sqlite,tenancy}/tests/{tx_deferred_test.go,tx_savepoint_test.go,batch_tx_failure_test.go,tx_resolver_test.go}` (events 3 + postgres 5 + sqlite 2 + tenancy 7). Events covers deferred-emission invariants layered on top of the existing `event_tx_test.go`: `TestEventTx_NoImmediateEmissionBetweenMutations` (subscriber sees zero events BETWEEN mutations inside a 3-Create tx under CallbackSync), `TestEventTx_NMutationsToNEventsOnCommit` (6 heterogeneous mutations — Create/Update/Upsert/Increment/SoftDelete/HardDelete across 2 tables — → exactly 6 events with matching actions on commit), `TestEventTx_EventsFireInInsertionOrder` (5 Creates → 5 events in Create-order PK). All use `WithCallbackMode(CallbackSync)` via a local `newSyncClient` helper for deterministic post-commit assertions. Postgres savepoint coverage adds 3 nested-tx tests: inner-rollback-preserves-outer, inner-commit-releases-to-outer, and `TestTxSavepoint_OuterRollbackDiscardsReleasedInner` (the §18.6 "released-savepoint callbacks still discarded if outer rolls back" invariant at row level — inner commits but outer errors, neither row persists). Postgres batch_tx_failure adds 2 tests building 201-input CreateMany on `categories` (UNIQUE(name) with inputs[200] colliding with pre-seeded row → [0:200] sub-batch persists, [200:201] fails): outside-Tx path asserts `Count == 200` (§9.5 non-atomic), inside-Tx path asserts `Count == 0` and pre-seed row untouched (atomic rollback via tx). Sqlite mirrors postgres savepoint coverage (2 tests); `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT` (not `ROLLBACK TRANSACTION TO SAVEPOINT` as the PRD §18 table lists — both valid SQLite grammar, stdlib emits the short form uniformly). Tenancy `tx_resolver_test.go` adds 7 tests pinning §29.6 resolver-call-accounting (tightened post FIX-055): `TestTx_Resolver_CalledExactlyOncePerMutation` (strict `count == N` for N mutations — replaces the original `>= N` lower bound), `TestTx_Resolver_ChainReusesCachedValue` (the headline FIX-055 invariant — one Create asserts `count == 1`, pre-fix was 3×), `TestTx_Resolver_SequentialOpsResolveIndependently` (two sequential Creates resolve twice — cache must not leak past op boundaries), `TestTx_Resolver_SkipTenancyDoesNotResolve` (`SkipTenancy:true` keeps `count == 0`), `TestTx_Resolver_StableAfterCommit` (two consecutive post-commit snapshots byte-equal under CallbackSync — no async resolver re-invocation), `TestTx_Resolver_NotCalledOnRollback` (pre-rollback count == post-rollback count), `TestTx_Resolver_UpdateBumpsCount` (mixed Create+Update tx invokes resolver for both ops — Update doesn't cache from Create). **Four phase-doc reconciliations documented inline:** (1) **§14.8 "tenant resolved once at Tx open" reconciled to "exactly once per mutation hook entry" per PRD §29.6.** The phase-14.md bullet conflicted with the PRD; landed runtime now matches the per-op contract literally post FIX-055; tests assert strict equality. (2) **Resolved 2026-04-24 via FIX-055.** Initial verification observed the runtime invoking the resolver 3× per chained `Create` (Create → Get → GetMany each independently called `c.resolveTenant(ctx)`). Filed as FIX-055 — ctx-cached single-resolve plumbing in `tenancy/tenancy.go` + every chaining template (`client/create/upsert/update/delete.go.tmpl`). PRD §29.6 ("once per mutation hook entry") now satisfied literally rather than approximately; phase 14.8 tests tightened from `>= N` to `== N`. (3) **PRD §18 table "ROLLBACK TRANSACTION TO SAVEPOINT" for SQLite reconciled to "ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT" as emitted.** stdlib emits the short form uniformly; SQLite accepts both per its grammar. (4) **FIX-054 surfaced during savepoint test bring-up.** First-cut tests used `"outer"`/`"inner"`/`"inner-release"` — postgres rejects reserved-word names, SQLite rejects hyphens. Filed and resolved as FIX-054 — `database/transaction.go` now validates savepoint names against `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*` + a cross-dialect reserved-word set, returning a sqlgen-level error instead of leaking dialect syntax errors. Tests use safe `sp_*` / `*_release` / `*_abort` names. **Deferred:** MySQL nested-tx coverage — the mysql example has no savepoint test scaffolding, and `database/transaction_test.go` pins the SQL strings at the runtime level; the sqlite test exercises the same stdlib code path end-to-end. **Phase-14 scope rule** (test-only) honoured for 14.8 itself; FIX-054 + FIX-055 work scoped under their own fix entries in `fixes.md`, not under 14.8. `make check` and `make check-examples` pass with 0 lint issues across all 7 example modules; all 17 new tests pass under `-race`. Next: 14.9 (nullable & array type-override round-trip).
